tatra ādau karmakāṇḍa ākhyaḥ pūrva khaṇḍaḥ prārabhyate om ajam ajaram anantam jñāna rūpam mahāntam śivam amalam anādim bhūta deha ādi hīnam devīm sarasvatīm ca eva manaḥ vāc karmabhiḥ sadā viṣṇu bhaktam mahātmānam naimiśa araṇyam āgatam śaunaka ādyāḥ mahābhāgāḥ naimiṣīyāḥ tapodhanāḥ munayaḥ ravi saṅkāśāḥ śāntāḥ yajña parāyaṇāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ sūta jānāsi sarvam tvam pṛcchāmaḥ tvām atas vayam devatānām hi kaḥ devaḥ īśvaraḥ pūjyaḥ eva kaḥ kasmāt pravartate dharmaḥ duṣṭa hantā ca kaḥ smṛtaḥ avatārāḥ ca ke tasya katham vaṃśa ādi sambhavaḥ nārāyaṇa kathāḥ sarvāḥ kathaya asmākam uttamāḥ sūtaḥ uvāca garuḍa uktam kaśyapāya purā vyāsāt śrutam mayā jagataḥ rakṣaṇa arthāya vāsudevaḥ ajaraḥ amaraḥ sa kumāra ādi rūpeṇa avatārān karoti ajaḥ naranārāyaṇaḥ bhūtvā turye tepe tapaḥ hariḥ dugdhaiḥ mahauṣadhaiḥ viprāḥ tena saṃjīvitāḥ prajāḥ āpyāyat surān anyān mohinyā mohayan striyā manu veda vidaḥ hi ādyāḥ sarve viṣṇu kalāḥ smṛtāḥ tasmāt sarga ādayaḥ jātāḥ saṃpūjyāḥ ca vrata ādinā purāṇam gāruḍam vyāsaḥ purā asau me abravīt idam iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye karmakāṇḍe etatpurāṇapravṛttihetunirūpaṇam nāma prathamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ katham vyāsena kathitam purāṇam gāruḍam tava sūtaḥ uvāca aham hi munibhiḥ sārdham gataḥ badarikāśramam tatra dṛṣṭaḥ mayā vyāsaḥ dhyāyamānaḥ para īśvaram tam praṇamya upaviṣṭaḥ aham pṛṣṭavān hi muni īśvaram sūtaḥ uvāca vyāsa brūhi hareḥ rūpam jagat sarga ādikam tatas manye dhyāyasi tam yasmāt tasmāt jānāsi tam vibhum evam pṛṣṭaḥ yathā prāha tathā viprāḥ nibodhata vyāsaḥ uvāca saha nārada dakṣa ādyaiḥ brahmā mām uktavān yathā sūtaḥ uvāca brahmā śrī gāruḍam puṇyam purāṇam sāra vācakam vyāsaḥ uvāca aham hi nāradaḥ dakṣaḥ bhṛgu ādyāḥ praṇipatya tam sāram brūhi iti papracchuḥ brahmāṇam brahma loka gam purāṇam gāruḍam sāram rudram ca mām yathā suraiḥ saha abravīt viṣṇuḥ tathā aham vyāsa vacmi te vyāsaḥ uvāca katham rudram suraiḥ sārdham abravīt vai hariḥ purā purāṇam gāruḍam sāram brūhi brahman mahā arthakam tatra dṛṣṭaḥ mayā rudraḥ dhyāyamānaḥ param padam pṛṣṭaḥ namaskṛtaḥ kim tvam devam dhyāyasi śaṅkara tvattaḥ nā anyam param devam jānāmi brūhi mām tatas sārāt sārataram tattvam śrotu kāmaḥ suraiḥ saha bhasma uddhūlita dehaḥ tu jaṭā maṇḍala maṇḍitaḥ viṣṇoḥ ārādhana artham me vrata caryā pitāmaha tam eva gatvā pṛcchāmaḥ sāram yam cintayāmi aham viṣṇum jiṣṇum padmanābham harim deha vivarjitam sahasra akṣam sahasra aṅghrim sahasra ūrum vara ānanam yasmin lokāḥ sphuranti ime jale śakunayaḥ yathā yasya trilokī jaṭhare kāṣṭhāḥ ca bāhavaḥ kukṣau samudrāḥ catvāraḥ tam devam cintayāmi aham anādiḥ ādiḥ viśvasya tam devam cintayāmi aham mukhāt agniḥ ca saṃjajñe tam devam cintayāmi aham padībhām yasya kṣitiḥ jātā śrotrābhyām ca tathā diśaḥ mūrdha bhāgāt divam yasya tam devam cintayāmi aham vaṃśānucaritam yasmāt tam devam cintayāmi aham yam dhyāyāmi aham etasmāt ūjāmaḥ sāram īkṣitum iti uktaḥ aham purā rudraḥ śvetadvīpa nivāsinam stutvā praṇamya tam viṣṇum śrotu kāma sthitaḥ suraiḥ sārān sārataram viṣṇum pṛṣṭavān tam praṇamya vai yathā papraccha mām vyāsaḥ stathā asau bhagavān bhavaḥ papraccha viṣṇum deva ādyaiḥ śṛṇvatām amaraiḥ saha kaiḥ dharmaiḥ kaiḥ ca niyamaiḥ kayā vā dharma pūjayā kena ācāreṇa tuṣṭaḥ syāt kim tad rūpam ca tasya vai kasmāt devāt jagat jātam jagat pālayate cakaḥ kīdṛśaiḥ avatāraiḥ ca kasmin yāti layam jagat parameśvara māhātmyam yukta yoga ādikam tathā tathā aṣṭādaśa vidyāḥ ca hariḥ rudram tatas abravīt śṛṇu rudra pravakṣyāmi brahmaṇā ca suraiḥ saha aham hi devaḥ devānām sarva loka īśvara īśvaraḥ aham dhyeyaḥ ca pūjyaḥ ca stutyaḥ uham statibhiḥ suraiḥ niyamaiḥ ca vrataiḥ tuṣṭaḥ ācāreṇa ca mānavaiḥ avatāraiḥ ca matsya ādyaiḥ pālayāmi akhilam jagat aham mantrāḥ ca mantra arthaḥ pūjā dhyāna paraḥ hi aham svarga ādīnām ca kartāham svarga ādīni aham eva ca yogī yogaḥ uham eva ādyaḥ purāṇāni aham eva ca jñātā śrotā tathā mantā vaktā vaktavyam eva ca dhyānam pūjā upahāraḥ aham maṇḍalāni aham eva ca itihāsāni aham rudra sarva vedāḥ hi aham śiva sarva jñānāni aham śambho brahma ātmā aham aham śiva aham sākṣāt sat ācāraḥ dharmaḥ aham vaiṣṇavaḥ hi aham varṇa āśramāḥ tathā ca aham tad dharmaḥ aham purātanaḥ aham sūryaḥ tathā candraḥ maṅgala ādīni aham tathā purā mām garuḍaḥ pakṣī tapasā ārādhayat bhuvi tuṣṭaḥ ūce varam brūhi mattaḥ vavre varam sa tu garuḍaḥ uvāca mama mātā ca vinatā nāgaiḥ dāsīkṛtā hare yathā aham deva tān jitvā ca amṛtam hi ānayāmi tat dāsyāt vimokṣayiṣyāmi yathā aham vāhanaḥ tava purāṇa saṃhitā kartā yathā aham syām tathā kurum viṣṇuḥ uvāca nāga dāsyāt mātaram tvam vinatām mokṣayiṣyasi deva ādīn sakalān jitvā ca amṛtam hi ānayiṣyasi mahā balaḥ vāhanaḥ tvam bhaviṣyasi viṣa ardanaḥ purāṇam mad prasādāt ca mama māhātmya vācakam yathā aham deva devānām śrīḥ khyātaḥ vinatā suta tathā khyātim purāṇeṣu gāruḍam gāruḍa eṣyati yathā aham kīrtanīyaḥ atha tathā tvam garuḍa ātmanā mām dhyātvā pakṣi mukhya idam purāṇam gada gāruḍam kaśyapaḥ gāruḍam śrutvā vṛkṣam dagdham ajīvayat svayam ca anya manāḥ bhūtvā jīvayat yakṣi om uṃ svāhā jāpī gāruḍī parā garuḍa uktam gāruḍam hi śṛṇu rudra mad ātmakam iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe śrīgaruḍamahāpurāṇotpattinirūpaṇam nāma dvitīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam sūtaḥ uvāca iti rudra abjajaḥ viṣṇoḥ śuśrāva brahmaṇaḥ muniḥ vyāsaḥ vyāsāt aham vakṣyā iham te śaunaka naimiṣe munīnām śṛṇvatām madhye sarga ādyam deva pūjanam varṇa āśrama ādi dharmāḥ ca dāna rāja ādi dharmakāḥ vyavahāraḥ vratam vaṃśāḥ vaidyakam sa nidānakam aṅgāni pralayaḥ dharma kāma artha jñānam uttamam sa prapañcam niṣprapañcam kṛtam viṣṇoḥ nigadyate purāṇe gāruḍe sarvam garuḍaḥ bhagavān atha vāsudeva prasādena sāmarthya atiśayaiḥ yutaḥ bhutvā hareḥ vāhanam ca sarga ādīnām ca kāraṇam devān vijitya garuḍaḥ hi amṛtā haraṇam tathā cakre kṣudhā hṛtam yasya brahmāṇḍam udare hareḥ yam dṛṣṭvā smṛta mātreṇa nāgāndīnām ca saṃkṣayaḥ kaśyapaḥ gāruḍāt vṛkṣam dagdham ca ajīvayat yatas garuḍaḥ sa hariḥ tena proktam śrī kaśyapāya ca tat śrīmat rāruḍam puṇyam sarva dam paṭhataḥ tava vakṣye vyāsam namaskṛtya śṛṇu śaunaka tat yathā iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe viṣayanirūpaṇam nāma tṛtīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam vaṃśānucaritam ca eva etat brūhi janārdana sarga sthiti laya antām tām viṣṇoḥ krīḍām purātanīm naranārāyaṇaḥ devaḥ vāsudevaḥ nirañjanaḥ tasmāt bhavati ca avyaktam tasmāt ātmā api jāyate śarīra grahaṇam pūrvam sṛṣṭi artham kurute prabhuḥ brahmā catur mukhaḥ bhūtvā rajaḥ mātra adhikaḥ sadā śarīra grahaṇam kṛtvā sṛjat etat carācaram brahmā bhūtvā sṛjat viṣṇuḥ jagat pāti hariḥ svayam brahmā tu sṛṣṭi kāle asmin ja madhya gatām mahīm daṃṣṭūya uddharati jñātvā vāra hīm āsthitaḥ tanūm deva ādi sargān vakṣye aham saṃkṣepāt śṛṇu śaṅkara tamaḥ mātrā tanuḥ tyaktā śaṅkara abhūt vibhāvarī yakṣa upakṣāṃsi tad dehe prītim āpuḥ tatas surāḥ rajaḥ mātrām tanum gṛhya manuṣyāḥ tu abhavan tatas rajaḥ mātrām tanu gṛhya kṣudh abhūt kopaḥ eva ca kṣudh tṛṣ kṣāmāḥ rākṣasāḥ rakṣaṇāt ca ye yakṣa ākhyāḥ jakṣaṇāt jñeyāḥ sarpāḥ vai keśa sarpaṇāt jātāḥ kopena bhūtāḥ te gandharvāḥ jajñire tatas ca eva antya mātaṅgān mahiṣa uṣṭra avikān tathā pūrva ādibhyaḥ mukhebhyaḥ tu ṛgveda ādyāḥ prajajñire ūrubhyām tu viśaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ śūdraḥ padibhāma jāyata brahma lokaḥ brāhmaṇānām śākraḥ kṣattriya janmanām mārutam ca viśām sthānam gāndharvam śūdra janmanām sthānam sapta ṛṣīṇām ca tathā eva vanavāsinām yatīnām akṣayam sthānam yadṛcchā gāminām sadā śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam kṛtvā iha amutra saṃsthānam prajā sargam tu mānasam atha amṛjat prajā kartṝn bhānasān tanayān prabhuḥ bhṛgum sanatkumāram ca rucim śraddhām tathā eva ca vasiṣṭham nāradam ca eva pitṝn barhiṣadaḥ tathā agniṣvāttān ca kavya ādān ājyapān ca sukālinaḥ upahūtān tathā dīpyān strīn ca mūrti vivarjitān caturaḥ mūrti yuktān ca aṅguṣṭhāt dakṣam īśvaram vāmām guṣṭhāt tasya bhāryām asṛjat padmasambhavaḥ tasyām tu janayāmāsa dakṣaḥ duhitaraḥ śubhāḥ rudra putrāḥ babhūvurhi asaṃkhyātāḥ mahā balāḥ bhṛgave ca dadau khyātim rūpeṇa apratimām śubhām bhṛgoḥ dhātāvidhātārau janayāmāsa sā śubhā tasyām vai janayāmāsa bala unmādau hariḥ svayam svāhām prādāt sa dakṣaḥ api śaśarīrāya vahnaye te ubhe brahma vādinyau menāyām tu himācalaḥ mainākam janayāmāsa gaurīm pūrvam tu yā satī devahūtim manuḥ tāsu ākūtim rucaye dadau prasūtim ca eva dakṣāya davahūtim ca kardame ruceḥ yajñaḥ dakṣiṇā abhūt dakṣiṇāyām ca yajñataḥ abhavan dvādaśa sutāḥ yāmāḥ nāma mahā balāḥ sṛṣṭavān dakṣaḥ uttamāḥ tasya jāmātaraḥ sarve yajñam jagmuḥ nimantritāḥ bhāryabhiḥ sahitāḥ sarve rudram devīm satīm vinā anāhūtā satī prāptā dakṣeṇa eva avamānitā tyaktā deham punar jātā menāyām tu himālayāt śambhoḥ bhāryā bhavat raurī tasyām jajñe vināyakaḥ kumāraḥ ca eva bhṛṅgīśaḥ kruddhaḥ rudraḥ pratāpavān vidhvaṃsya yajñam dakṣam tu tam śaśāpa pinākadhṛk iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe prajākartrādisṛṣṭiḥ nāma pañcamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam uttānapādāt abhavat surucyām uttamaḥ sutaḥ dhruvasya tanayaḥ śliṣṭiḥ mahā bala parākramaḥ ripoḥ putraḥ tathā śrīmān cākṣuṣaḥ kīrtitaḥ manuḥ ruruḥ tasya sutaḥ śrīmān aṅgaḥ tasya api ca ātmajaḥ a dharṃma kārī munibhiḥ ca kuśaiḥ hataḥ hrasvaḥ atimātraḥ kṛṣṇa aṅgaḥ niṣīda iti tatas abruvan niṣādaḥ tena vai jātaḥ bindhya śaila nivāsakaḥ tasmāt tasya sutaḥ jātaḥ viṣṇoḥ mānasa rūpadhṛk dudoha pṛthivīm rājā prajānām jīvanāya hi antardhānaḥ pṛthoḥ putraḥ tahavirdhānaḥ tad ātmajaḥ prajāpati tvam saṃprāpya bhāryā teṣām ca māriṣa abhavat hava śāpena tasyām dakṣaḥ abhavat tatas asṛjat manasā dakṣaḥ prajāḥ pūrvam catur vodhāḥ nārada uktāḥ bhuvaḥ ca antam gatāḥ jñātum ca na āgatāḥ dakṣa putra sahasram ca teṣu naṣṭeṣu sṛṣṭavān śavala aśvāḥ te api gatāḥ bhrātṝṇām padavīm hara dakṣaḥ kruddhaḥ śaśāpa atha nāradam janma ca āpsyasi yajñe dhvaste atha dakṣaḥ api śaśāpra ugram maheśvaram stutvā tvām upacāraiḥ ca pūjayiṣyanti śaṅkara asiknyām (mahiṣyām janayāmāsa dakṣaḥ duhitaraḥ hi atha ṣaṣṭim kanyāḥ rūpa yutāḥ dve ca eva aṅgirase dadau dve prādāt sa kṛśāśvāya daśa dharṃmāya ca api atha caturdaśa kaśyapāya aṣṭāviṃśā atimindave pradadau bahuputrāya suprabhām bhāminīm tathā manoramām bhānumatīm viśālām bahudām atha dakṣaḥ prādāt mahādeva catasraḥ ariṣṭanemaye sa kṛśāśvāya ca prādāt suprajām ca tathā jayām dharṃma patnyaḥ samākhyātāḥ kaśyapasya vadāmi aham adityām kaśyapāt ca eva sūryāḥ dvādaśa jajñire saṃhrādaḥ ca avamaḥ teṣām prahrādaḥ viṣṇu tatparaḥ īdṛś sadṛś tathā anyādṛś tatas pratisadṛś tathā vidhāraṇaḥ ca durmedhāḥ ayam eka gaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ hetumān prasavaḥ tadvat surabhaḥ ca mahā yaśāḥ etat sarvam hare rūpam rājānaḥ dānavāḥ surāḥ sūrya ādi parivāreṇa manu ādyāḥ ījire harim iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe uttānapādavaṃśādivarṇanam nāma ṣaṣṭhaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam sūrya ādi pūjanam brūhi kṛtam svāyambhuva ādibhiḥ bhukti mukti pradam sāram vyāsa saṃkṣepataḥ param sūrya ādi pūjām vakṣyāmi dharṃma kāma ādi kārikām om sūrya āsanāya namaḥ om namaḥ sūrya mūrtaye om somāya namaḥ om maṅgalāya namaḥ om budhāya namaḥ om bṛhaspataye namaḥ om śukrāya namaḥ om śanaiścarāya namaḥ om rāhave namaḥ om ketave namaḥ om tejaḥ caṇḍāya namaḥ dīpakam ca naskāram pradakṣiṇa visarjane sūrya ādīnām sadā kuryāt iti mantraiḥ vṛṣadhvaja om hrāṃśivāya namaḥ om hrāṃ śiva mūrtaye śivāya namaḥ om hrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ om hrīṃ śirase svāhā om hrūm śikhāyai vaṣaṭ om hrai kavacāya hum om netratrayāya vauṣaṭ om hraḥ astrāya namaḥ om hrāṃ sadyojātaye namaḥ om hrīṃ vāmadevāya namaḥ om aghorāya namaḥ om tatpuruṣāya namaḥ om hrauṃ īśānāya namaḥ om hrīṃ gauryai namaḥ om hrauṃ gurubhyaḥ namaḥ om hrauṃ indrāya namaḥ om hrauṃ caṇḍāya namaḥ om hrāṃ aghorāya namaḥ om vāsudeva āsanāya namaḥ om nārāyaṇāya namaḥ om hrāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ om kaṃ ṭaṃ paṃ śam vainateyāya namaḥ om jaṃ khaṃ raṃ sudarśa nāya namaḥ om khaṃ ṭhaṃ phaṃ ṣaṃ gadaiyau namaḥ om vaṃ laṃ maṃ pāñcajanyāya namaḥ om gaṃ ḍaṃ vaṃ saṃ puṣṭyai namaḥ om sam daṃ laṃ śrīvatsāya namaḥ om ṭhaṃ caṃ bhaṃ yaṃ kaustubhāya namaḥ om indra ādi dikpālebhyaḥ namaḥ om viṣvaksenāya namaḥ viṣṇu śaktyāḥ sarasvatyāḥ pūjām śṛṇu śubha pradām om hrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ om hrīṃ śirase namaḥ om hrūm śikhāyai namaḥ om hraiṃ kavacāya namaḥ om netratrayāya namaḥ om hraḥ astrāya namaḥ śraddhā ṛddhiḥ kalā medhā tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭiḥ prabhā matiḥ om hrīṅkāra ādyāḥ namaḥ antāḥ ca sarasvatyāḥ ca śaktayaḥ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ padma sthāyāḥ sarasvatyāḥ āsana ādyam prakalpayet sūrya ādīnām svakaiḥ prantraiḥ pavitra ārohaṇam tathā iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe sūrya ādīnām sarasvatyāḥ ca pūjanam nāma saptamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam bhūmiṣṭhe maṇḍape snātvā maṇḍale viṣṇum arcayet pañca raṅgika cūrṇena vajra nābham tu maṇḍalam ṣoḍaśaiḥ koṣṭhakaiḥ tatra saṃmitam rudra kārayet caturtha pañca koṇeṣu sūtra pātam tu kārayet koṇa sūtrāt ubhayatas koṇāḥ ye tatra saṃsthitāḥ teṣu ca eva prakurvīta sūtra pātam vicakṣaṇaḥ prathamā nābhiḥ uddiṣṭā madhye rekhā prasaṃgame antareṣu ca sarveṣu aṣṭau ca eva tunābhayaḥ pūrva madhyama nābhi bhyāmatham sūtram tu bhrāmayet antarā sa dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ pāda ūnam bhrāmayet hara anena nābhi sūtrasya karṇikām bhrāmayet śiva karṇikāyāḥ dvi bhāgena kesarāṇi vicakṣaṇaḥ sarveṣu mānena anena suvrata padmāni tāni kurvīta deśikaḥ para mā artha vid ādi sūtra vibhāgena dvārāṇi parikalpayet karṇikām pīta varṇena sita rakta ādi kesaraiḥ kṛṣṇa varṇena rajasā caturaśram prapūrayet dvārāṇi śukla varṇena rekhāḥ pañca ca maṇḍale kṛtvā eva maṇḍalam ca ādau nyāsam kṛtvā arcayet harim hṛd madhye tu nyaset viṣṇum kaṇṭhe saṅkarṣaṇam tathā aham viṣṇuḥ iti dhyātvā karṇikāyām nyaset harim śrīdharam rudra koṇeṣu indra ādīn dikṣu vinyaset iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe viṣṇupūjopayogivajranābhamaṇḍalanirūpaṇam nāma aṣṭamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam samaye dīkṣitaḥ śiṣyaḥ baddha netraḥ tu vāsasā aṣṭa āhuti śatam tasya mūlamantreṇa homayet dviguṇam putrake homam triguṇam sādhake matam nirvāṇa deśike rudra caturguṇam udāhṛtam guru viṣṇu dvija strīṇām hantā badhyaḥ tvam a dīkṣitaiḥ atha dīkṣām pravakṣyāmi dharma adharma kṣayaṅkarīm upaveśya bahis śiṣyān dhāraṇam teṣu kārayet vāyavyā kalayā rudra śoṣyamāṇān vicintayet āgreyyāḥ dahyamānān ca plāvitān ambhasā punar tejaḥ teja asi tam jīvam ekīkṛtya samākṣipet cintayet dyomni śarīre anyat tu kāraṇam ekaikam yaḥ jayet tatra kṣetrajñam deha kāraṇāt utpādya yojayet paścāt ekaikam vṛṣabhadhvaja hastam padmam samākhyātam patrāṇi aṅgulayaḥ smṛtāḥ tam hastam pātayet mūrdhni śiṣyasya tu samāhitaḥ naśyanti sparśanāt tasya pātakāni akhilāni ca puṣpam nipatitam yatra mūrdhnaḥ devasya śārṅgiṇaḥ tad nāma kārayet tasya strīṇām nāma aṅkitam svayam śūdrāṇām dāsa saṃyuktam kārayet tu vicakṣaṇaḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe nāma navamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam śyā ādi pūjām sthaṇḍila ādiṣu siddhaye śrīṃ hrīṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ śrāṃ śrīṃ śrūṃ śraiṃ śrauṃ śraḥ kramāt hṛdayam ca śiraḥ śikhām lakṣmīm aṅgāni ca ekasmin koṇe durgām gaṇam gurum kṣetrapālam atha agni ādau homān juhāva kāma bhāj om ghaṃ ṭaṃ ḍaṃ haṃ śrī mahālakṣmyai namaḥ anena pūjayet lakṣmīm pūrva ukta parivārakaiḥ om om sarasvatyai namaḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe lakṣmyarcananirūpaṇam nāma daśamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam hariḥ uvāca jīvam utkṣipya mūrdhanyam nābhyām vyomni niveśayet dvādaśa akṣara bījena ukta bījaiḥ anantaram ṣaḍaṅgena tatas kuryāt sākṣāt yena hariḥ bhavet kramāt ca etāni bījāni tarjanī ādiṣu vinya set tatas mūrdha akṣi vakkreṣu kaṇṭhe ca hṛdaye tathā pāṇyoḥ ṣaḍaṅga bījāni nyasya kāye tatas nyaset hṛdaye cintayet pūrvam yogapīṭham samāhitaḥ āgneya ādau ca pūrva ādau adharma ādīn ca vinyaset ebhiḥ paricchanna tanum pīṭha bhūtam tad ātmakam tatas vidyāt saraḥ jātam dala aṣṭa sama diś dalam sita abjam śata patra āḍhyam viprakīrṇa ūrdhva karṇikam dhyātvā vedādinā paścāt sūrya soma anala ātmanām tatas pūrva ādi diś saṃsthāḥ śaktīḥ keśava gocarāḥ vimalā ādyāḥ nyaset aṣṭau navamīm karṇikā gatām manasā āvāhya tatra īśam harim śārṅgam nyaset punar hṛdaya ādīni pūrva ādi catur diś dala yogataḥ saṅkarṣaṇa ādi bījāni pūrva ādi krama yogataḥ sudarśanam sahasra aram dakṣiṇe dvāri vinyaset dvāri attare gadām nyasya śaṅkham koṇeṣu vinyaset deva dakṣiṇatas śārṅgam vāme ca eva sudhīḥ nyaset sarvam dhyātvā iti saṃpūjya mudrāḥ sandarśayet tatas añjaliḥ prathamā mudrā kṣipram deva prasādhanī vandanī hṛdaya āsaktāt sārdham dakṣiṇatas unnatā savyasya tasya ca aṅguṣṭhaḥ yaḥ saḥ prakīrtitaḥ tisraḥ sādhāraṇāḥ hi etāḥ mūrti bhedena kalpitāḥ navamī iyam smṛtā mudrā varāha abhimatā sadā muṣṭi dvayam atho baddhā evam eva anupūrvaśas suram ādyam dvitīyam ca upāntyam ca antyam eva ca praṇavaḥ tat sat iti etat hum kṣaiṃ bhūḥ iti mantrakāḥ kaṃ ṭaṃ paṃ śam garutmān syāt jaṃ khaṃ vaṃ ca sudarśanam ṣaṃ caṃ phaṃ ṣaṃ gadā devī vaṃ laṃ maṃ kṣaṃ ca śaṅkhakam ghaṃ ḍhaṃ bhaṃ haṃ bhavet śrīḥ ca gaṃ jaṃ vaṃ śam ca puṣṭikā ghaṃ vaṃ ca vanamālā syāt śrī vatsam daṃ sam bhavet chaṃ ḍaṃ paṃ yaṃ kaustubhaḥ proktaḥ ca anantaḥ hi aham eva ca iti aṅgāni yathāyogam devadevasya vai daśā garuḍaḥ ambuja saṃkāśaḥ gadā ca eva asita ākṛtiḥ puṣṭiḥ śirīṣa puṣpa ābhā lakṣmīḥ kāñcana sannibhā pūrṇa candra nibhaḥ śaṅkhaḥ kaustubhaḥ tu aruṇa dyutiḥ cakram sūrya sahasra ābham śrīvatsaḥ kunda sannibhaḥ vidyut rūpāṇi ca astrāṇi yāni na uktāni varṇataḥ arghya pādya ādi vai dadyāt puṇḍarīkākṣa vidyayā iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe navavyūhārcanam nāma ekādaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam om namaḥ ityādau saṃsmṛtiḥ paramātmanaḥ yaṃ raṃ vaṃ lam iti kāya śuddhiḥ tatas tdṛdistha yogapīṭha pūjā om anantāya namaḥ om dharmāya namaḥ om jñānāya namaḥ om vairāgyāya namaḥ om aiśvaryāya namaḥ om adharṃmāya namaḥ om ajñānāya namaḥ om avairāgyāya namaḥ om anaiśvaryāya namaḥ om padmāya namaḥ om āditya maṇḍalāya namaḥ om candra maṇḍalāya namaḥ om vahni maṇḍalāya namaḥ om vimalāyai namaḥ om utkarṣiṇyai namaḥ om jñānāyai namaḥ om kriyāyai namaḥ om yogāyai namaḥ om satyāyai namaḥ om īśānāyai namaḥ om saṃga upāṅgāya hareḥ āsanāya namaḥ tatas karṇikāyām aṃ vāsudevāya namaḥ āṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ īm śirase namaḥ ūm śikhāyai namaḥ aim kavacāya namaḥ aum netratrayāya namaḥ aḥ phaṭ astrāya namaḥ ām saṅkarṣaṇāya namaḥ aṃ pradyumnāya namaḥ aḥ aniruddhāya namaḥ om aḥ nārāyaṇāya namaḥ om namaḥ om hum viṣṇave namaḥ kṣaiṃ narasiṃhāya bhūḥ varāhya kaṃ śrīvatsāṃkaḥ tathā eva ca prakṛtiḥ kaustubha grīvaḥ pīta ambara dharaḥ tathā sukapolaḥ vibhuḥ jiṣṇuḥ bhrājiṣṇuḥ ca aiṣudhīḥ tathā nihantā pūtanāyāḥ ca bhāskara anta vināśanaḥ kaṃsa dānava bhettā ca cāṇūrasya pramardakaḥ akrūraḥ krūrarūpaḥ ca akrūrapriyavanditaḥ aham kārā upamāḥ cittam gaganam pṛthivī jalam vāyuḥ cakṣuḥ tathā śrotram jihvā ca ghrāṇam eva ca śaṅkaraḥ ca eva sarvaḥ ca kṣānti daḥ kṣānti kṛt naraḥ kīrtiḥ deptiḥ kṣamā kāntiḥ bhaktaḥ ca eva dayā parā dānam dātā ca kartā ca devadeva priyaḥ śuciḥ śucimān sukha daḥ mokṣaḥ kāmaḥ ca arthaḥ sahasrapād sahasra śīrṣā vaidyaḥ ca mokṣadvāram tathā eva ca prajādvāram sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrakaraḥ eva ca śukraḥ ca sukirīṭī ca sugrīvaḥ kaustubhaḥ tathā pradyumnaḥ ca aniruddhaḥ ca hayagrīvaḥ ca sūkaraḥ śaraṇyaḥ ca eva nityaḥ ca buddhaḥ muktaḥ śarīra bhṛt khara dūṣaṇa hantā ca rāvaṇasya pramardanaḥ narāntaka antakaḥ ca eva devāntaka vināśanaḥ duṣṭa asura nihantā ca śambarāriḥ tathā eva ca narakasya nihantā ca triśīrṣasya vināśanaḥ udānaḥ ca samānaḥ ca bheṣajam ca bhiṣaj tathā hasta indriya vihīnaḥ ca pādābhyām ca vivarjitaḥ pāyu upastha vihīnaḥ ca maru tāpa vivarjitaḥ pṛthivyā ca vihīnaḥ ca śabdena ca vivarjitaḥ sūkṣmaḥ ca eva su sūkṣmaḥ ca sthūlāt sthūlataraḥ tathā viśāradaḥ bala adhyakṣaḥ sarvasya kṣobhakaḥ tathā prakṛteḥ kṣobhakaḥ ca eva mahataḥ kṣobhakaḥ tathā bhūtānām kṣobhakaḥ ca eva buddheḥ ca kṣomakaḥ tathā indriyāṇām kṣobhakaḥ ca viṣaya kṣobhakaḥ tathā tvacā na gamyaḥ kūrṃmaḥ ca jihvā grāhyaḥ tathā eva ca grāṇa indriya a gamyaḥ eva vācā a grāhyaḥ tathā eva ca aham buddhyā tathā grāhyaḥ cetasā grāhyāḥ eva ca śaṅkhapāṇiḥ ca avyayaḥ ca gadā pāṇiḥ tathā eva ca śārṅgapāṇiḥ ca kṛṣṇaḥ ca jñāna mūrtiḥ parantapaḥ tapasvī jñāna gamyaḥ hi jñānī jñāna vid eva ca jñeyaḥ ca jñeya hīnaḥ ca jñaptiḥ caitanya rūpakaḥ bhāvaḥ bhāvyaḥ bhava karaḥ bhāvanaḥ bhava nāśanaḥ upendraḥ ca nṛsiṃhaḥ ca śauriḥ ca eva janārdanaḥ āraṇeyaḥ bṛhadbhānuḥ bṛhaddīptiḥ tathā eva ca dāmodaraḥ tnikālaḥ ca kālajñaḥ kālavarjitaḥ trisandhyaḥ dvāparam tretā prajādvāram tri vikramaḥ vikramaḥ daṇḍa hastaḥ ca hi ekadaṇḍī tridaṇḍa dhṛk sāma bhedaḥ tathā upāyaḥ sāmarūpī ca sāmagaḥ sāmaveda ūḍhī atharvaḥ ca sukṛtaḥ suta rūpaṇaḥ ṛk rūpī ca eva ṛgvedaḥ ṛgvedeṣu pratiṣṭhitaḥ catuṣpād ca dvipād ca eva smṛtiḥ nyāyaḥ yamaḥ balī sannyāsī sannayāsaḥ catur āśramaḥ eva ca śīla daḥ śīla sampannaḥ śīla parivarjitaḥ mokṣaḥ adhyātma samāviṣṭaḥ stutiḥ stotā ca pūjakaḥ karaṇam ca eva vācyam ca eva tu vācakaḥ vettā vyākaraṇam ca eva vākyam ca eva ca vākya vid vākya gamyaḥ tīrtha vāsī tīrthaḥ ca tīrtha vid tīrtha ādi bhūtaḥ sāṃkhyaḥ ca niruktam tu adhidaivatam praṇavaḥ praṇava īśaḥ ca praṇavena pravanditaḥ praṇavena ca lakṣyaḥ vai gāyatrī ca gadādharaḥ śālagrāma nivāsī ca śālagrāmaḥ tathā eva ca jalaśāyī yogaśāyī śeṣaśāyī kuśeśayaḥ prajāpatiḥ śāśvataḥ ca kāmyaḥ kāmayitā virāj samrāj pūṣā tathā svargaḥ ratha sthaḥ sārathiḥ balam dhanī dhana pradaḥ dhanyaḥ yādavānām hite rataḥ sārasvataḥ mahā bhīṣmaḥ pārijāta haraḥ tathā amṛtasya pradātā ca kṣīrodaḥ kṣīram eva ca śipiviṣṭaḥ prasannaḥ ca sarva loka ārti nāśanaḥ śrotrā śrotra niyantā ca śrotavyaḥ śravaṇam tathā tvac sthitaḥ ca sparśayitvā spṛśyam ca sparśanam tathā rūpa draṣṭā ca cakṣuḥ sthaḥ niyantā cakṣuṣaḥ tathā dṛśyam ca eva tu jihvā sthaḥ rasajñaḥ ca niyāmakaḥ vāc sthaḥ vaktā ca vaktavyaḥ vacanam vāc niyāmakaḥ prāṇi sthaḥ śilpa kṛt śilpaḥ hastayoḥ ca niyāmakaḥ niyantā pādayoḥ ca eva pādya bhāj ca visarga kṛt dhūmakṛt dhūma rūpaḥ ca devakīputraḥ uttamaḥ aṭṭahāsapriyaḥ ca eva sarvādhyakṣaḥ kṣaraḥ akṣaraḥ acyutaḥ ca eva satyeśaḥ satyāyāḥ ca priyaḥ varaḥ rukmiṇyāḥ ca patiḥ ca eva rukmiṇyāḥ vallabhaḥ tathā gopīnām vallabhaḥ ca eva puṇyaślokaḥ ca viśrutaḥ grāhasya vinihantā ca grāmī rakṣakaḥ tathā viśvarūpaḥ viśālākṣaḥ daityasūdanaḥ eva ca suṣupti sthaḥ suṣuptiḥ ca sthānam sthāna antaḥ eva ca jagatsthaḥ ca eva jāgartā sthānam jāgaritam tathā vijñānam vedya rūpam ca jīvaḥ jīvayitā tathā bhuvana adhipatiḥ ca eva bhuvanānām niyāmakaḥ paramānanda rūpī ca dharṃmāṇām ca pravartakaḥ sulabhaḥ durlabhaḥ ca eva prāṇāyāma paraḥ tathā pratyāhāraḥ dhārakaḥ ca pratyāhāra karaḥ tathā agrāhaḥ ca eva gauraḥ ca sarvaḥ śuciḥ abhiṣṭutaḥ vaṣaṭkāraḥ vaṣaṭ vauṣaṭ svadhā svāhā ratiḥ tathā paktā nandayitā abhoktā boddhā bhāvayitā tathā jñāna ātmā ca eva deha ātmā bhū mā sarva īśvara īśvaraḥ nadī nandī ca nandīśaḥ bhārataḥ tarunāśanaḥ cakrapaḥ śrīpatiḥ ca eva nṛpāṇām cakravartinām īśaḥ ca sarva devānām dvārakā saṃsthitaḥ tathā puṣkaraḥ puṣkarādhyakṣaḥ puṣkaradvīpaḥ eva ca nirākāraḥ nirnimittaḥ nirātaṅkaḥ nirāśrayaḥ devasya viṣṇoḥ īśaśya sarva pāpa vināśanam paṭhan dvijaḥ ca viṣṇu tvam kṣatriyaḥ jayam āpnuyāt vaiśyaḥ dhanam sukham śūdraḥ viṣṇu bhakti samanvitaḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe śrīviṣṇusahasranāmastotranirūpaṇam nāma pañcadaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam śṛṇu rudra hareḥ dhyānam saṃsāra taru nāśanam sthūla deha vihīnam ca cakṣuṣā parivarjitam buddhyā vihīnam deveśam cetasā parivarjitam vyāna ākhya vāyu hīnam vai prāṇa dharṃma vivarjitam punar sūri arcanam vakṣye yat uktam bhṛgave purā om khakholkāya namaḥ om khakholkāya tridaśāya namaḥ om vici ṭha ṭha śirase namaḥ om jñānine ṭha ṭha śikhāyai namaḥ om sahasraraśmaye ṭha ṭha kavacāya namaḥ agni prākāra mantraḥ ayam sūryasya agha vināśanaḥ sakalīkaraṇam kuryāt rāyatryā bhāskarasya ca dharṃma ātmane ca pūrvasmin yamāḥ yā iti ca dakṣiṇe śyāma piṅgalam aiśānyām āgneyyām dīkṣitam yajet vajrapāṇim ca nairṛtyām bhūḥ bhuvaḥ svar ca vāyave om candrāya nakṣatra adhipataye namaḥ om aṅgārakāya kṣitisutāya mantreṇa tatas sūryam visarjayet iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe haridhyānasūryārcanayornirūpaṇam nāma ṣoḍaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam punar sūrya vāsukiḥ ca eva nāgān ityādi pūjayet iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe sūryārcana vidhiḥ nāma saptadaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam sūtaḥ uvāca garuḍa uktam samākhyātā pūjā viṣṇoḥ mahātmanaḥ iṃ yaḥ paṭhate adhyāyam viṣṇu pūjā prakāśakam iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe śrīdharācanavidhiḥ nāma triṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam bhūyas evam jagannātha pūjām kathaya me prabho arcanam viṣṇu devasya vakṣyāmi vṛṣabhadhvaja tat śṛṇuṣva mahābhāga bhukti mukti pradam śubham kṛtvā snānam tatas sandhyām tatas yāga gṛham vrajet mūlamantram ca devasya śṛṇu rudra vadāmi te ayam mantraḥ sureśasya viṣṇoḥ īśasya vācakaḥ aṅganyāsam tatas kuyyāt debhiḥ mantrauḥ vicakṣaṇaḥ om hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ om hīṃ śirase svāhā om hūm śikhāyai vaṣaṭ om haiṃ kavacāya hum om hauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ om haḥ astrāya phaṭ iti mantraḥ samākhyātaḥ mayā te prabhaviṣṇunā nyāsam kṛtvā ātmanaḥ mudrām darśayet vijita ātmavān tatas dhyāyet param viṣṇu tdṛt koṭara samāśritam aham viṣṇuḥ iti dhyātvā kṛtvā vai śodhana ādikam yaṃ kṣaiṃ ram iti bījaiḥ ca kaṭhinī kṛtya nāmabhiḥ tatra pūrva ukta rūpam tu bhāvayitvā vṛṣadhvaja āvāhya pūjayet sarvāḥ devatāḥ āsanasya yāḥ mantraiḥ ebhiḥ mahādeva tat mantram śṛṇu śaṅkara viṣṇo āsana devatāḥ āgacchata om samasta parivārāya acyutāya namaḥ om dhātre namaḥ om vidhātre namaḥ om gaṅgāyai namaḥ om yamunāyai namaḥ om śaṅkhanidhaye namaḥ om caṇḍāya namaḥ om pracaṇḍāya namaḥ om dvāra śriyai namaḥ om ādhāra śaktyai namaḥ om kūrṃmāya namaḥ om anantāya namaḥ om śriyai namaḥ om dharṃmāya namaḥ om jñānāya namaḥ om vairāgyāya namaḥ om aiśvaryāya namaḥ om adharṃmāya namaḥ om ajñānāya namaḥ om avairāgyāya namaḥ om anaiśvaryāya namaḥ om saṃ sattvāya namaḥ om raṃ rajase namaḥ om taṃ tamase namaḥ om kaṃ kandāya namaḥ om naṃ nālāya namaḥ om lāṃ padmāya namaḥ om aṃ arka maṇḍalāya namaḥ om soṃ soma maṇḍalāya namaḥ om vaṃ vahni maṇḍalāya namaḥ om vimalāyai namaḥ om utkarṣiṇyai namaḥ om jñānāyai namaḥ om kriyāyai namaḥ om yogāyai namaḥ om satyāyai namaḥ om īśānāyai namaḥ om anugrahāyai namaḥ pūjayitvā tatas viṣṇum sṛṣṭi saṃhāra kāriṇam āvāhya maṇḍale rudra pūjayet pa rameśvaram mudrām pradarśayet paścāt arghya ādīn arpayet tatas snānām kuryāt tatas vastram dadyāt ācamanam tatas aṅga ādīnām sva mantraiḥ ca pūjām kurvīta sādhakaḥ mantrān śṛṇu trinetra tvam kathyamānān mayā adhunā om hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ om hīṃ śirase namaḥ om hūm śikhāyai namaḥ om haiṃ kavacāya namaḥ om netratrayāya namaḥ om haḥ astrāya namaḥ om śriyai namaḥ om śaṅkāya namaḥ om padmāya namaḥ om cakrāya namaḥ om gadāyai namaḥ om śrīvatsāya namaḥ om kaustubhāya namaḥ om vanamālāyai namaḥ om pītāmbarāya namaḥ om khaḍgāya namaḥ om musalāya namaḥ om pāśāya namaḥ om aṅkuśāya namaḥ śārṅgāya namaḥ om śarāya namaḥ om brahmaṇe namaḥ om nārādāya namaḥ om bhāgavatebhyaḥ namaḥ om indrāya sura adhipataye sa vāhana parivārāya namaḥ om yamāya preta adhipataye sa vāhana parivārāya namaḥ om varuṇāya jala adhipataye sa vādana parivārāya namaḥ om vāyave prāṇa adhipataye sa vāhana parivārāya namaḥ om somāya nakṣatra adhipataye sa vāhana parivārāya namaḥ om īśānāya vidyā adhipataye sa vāhana parivārāya namaḥ om anantāya nāga adhipataye sa vāhana parivārāya namaḥ om brahmaṇe loka adhipataye sa vāhana parivārāya namaḥ om vajrāya hum phaṭ namaḥ om śaktyai hum phaṭ namaḥ om daṇḍāya hum phaṭ namaḥ om khaḍgāya hum phaṭ namaḥ om pāśāya hum phaṭ namaḥ om dhvajāya hum phaṭ namaḥ om gadāyai hum phaṭ namaḥ om triśūlāya hum phaṭ namaḥ om cakrāya hum phaṭ namaḥ om padmāya hum phaṭ namaḥ om vaiṃ viṣvaksenāya namaḥ ebhiḥ mantraiḥ mahādeva pūjyāḥ aṅga ādayaḥ naraiḥ pūjayitvā mahātmānam viṣṇum brahma svarūpiṇam stuvīta ca anayā stutyā paramātmānam avyayam grasiṣṇave namaḥ ca eva namaḥ pralaya śāyine munīnām prabhave nityam yakṣāṇām prabhaviṣṇave jiṣṇave sarva devānām sarvagāya mahātmane śaraṇyāya su rūpāya dharma kāma artha dāyine elam tu pūjayet viṣṇum mūlamantreṇa śaṅkara etat yaḥ ca paṭhet vidvān viṣṇu bhaktaḥ pumān hara iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe viṣṇupūjāvidhiḥ nāma ekatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam maṅgalyam maṅgalam divyam rahasyam kāma dam param vāsudevaḥ dhruvaḥ śuddhaḥ sarva vyāpī nirañjanaḥ saḥ eva māyāyāḥ deva pañcadhā saṃsthitaḥ hariḥ loka anugraha kṛt viṣṇuḥ sarva duṣṭa vināśanaḥ nārāyaṇa sva rūpeṇa pañcadhā hi advayaḥ sthitaḥ eteṣām vācakān mantrān etān śṛṇu vṛṣadhvaja om aṃ vāsudevāya namaḥ om āṃ saṃkarṣaṇāya namaḥ om aṃ pradyumnāya namaḥ om aniruddhāya namaḥ om om nārāyaṇāya namaḥ pañca mantrāḥ samākhyātāḥ devānām vācakāḥ tava adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi pañcatattva arcanam śubham ādau snānam prakurvīta snātvā sandhyām samācaret arcana āgāram āsādya prakṣālya ārṅghya ādikam tathā ācamya upaviśet prājñaḥ baddha āsanam abhīpsitam śoṣaṇa ādi tatas kuryāt aṃ kṣaiṃ ram iti mantrakaiḥ sāmānyam kaṭhinīkṛtya ca aṇḍam utpādayet tatas vibhidya aṇḍam tatas hi aṇḍe bhāvayet parameśvaram tatas saṃkarṣaṇam devam ātmānam cintayet prabhum pradyumnam aniruddham ca śrīmat nārāyaṇam tatas indra ādīn ca surān tasmāt devadevāt samutthitān vyāpakam mūlamantreṇa ca aṅganyāsam tatas param aṅgamantraiḥ mahādeva tān mantrān śṛṇu suvrata om āṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ om īṃ śirase namaḥ om ūm śikhāyai namaḥ om aim kavacāya namaḥ om aum netratrayāya namaḥ om aḥ astrāya phaṭ om samasta parivārāya acyutāya namaḥ om dhātre namaḥ om vidhātre namaḥ om ādhāra śaktayai namaḥ om kūrmāya namaḥ om anantāya namaḥ om pṛthivyai namaḥ om dharmāya namaḥ om dharmāya namaḥ om jñānāya namaḥ om vairāgyāya namaḥ om aiśvaryāya namaḥ om ajñānāya namaḥ om anaiśvaryāya namaḥ om aṃ arka maṇḍalāya namaḥ om soṃ soma maṇa āḍalāya namaḥ om vaṃ vahni maṇḍalāya namaḥ om vaṃ vāsudevāya para brahmaṇe śivāya tejaḥ rūpāya vyāpine sarva deva adhidevāya namaḥ om pāñcajanyāya namaḥ om sudarśa vanāya namaḥ om gadāyai namaḥ om padmāya namaḥ om śriyai namaḥ om hriyai namaḥ om puṣṭyai namaḥ om gītyai namaḥ om śaktyai namaḥ om prītyai namaḥ om indrāya namaḥ om agnaye namaḥ om yamāya namaḥ om nirṛtaye namaḥ om varuṇāya namaḥ om vāyave namaḥ om somāya namaḥ om īśānāya namaḥ om anantāya namaḥ om brahmaṇe namaḥ om viṣvaksenāya namaḥ ete mantrāḥ samākhyātāḥ tava rudra samāsatas pūjā ca eva prakartavyā maṇḍale svastika ādike om padmāya namaḥ aṅganyāsam ca kṛtvā tu mudrāḥ sarvāḥ pradaśayat āsanam pūjayet paścāt āvāhya vidhivat naraḥ dvāre dhātuḥ vidhātuḥ ca pūjā kāryā vṛṣadhvaja garuḍam pūjayet agre vāsudevasya śaṅkara āgneya ādiṣu arcayet vai adharma ādi catuṣṭayam pūrva ādi padma patreṣu pūjyāḥ saṃkarṣaṇa ādayaḥ pāñcajanya ādayaḥ pūjyāḥ aiśānī ādiṣu saṃsthitāḥ śaktayaḥ ca eva pūrva ādau devadevasya śaṅkara indra ādayaḥ lokapālāḥ pūjyāḥ pūrva ādiṣu sthitāḥ adhas nāga tu brahmāṇam pūjayet sudhīḥ iti sthāna kramaḥ jñeyaḥ maṇḍale śaṅkara tvayā āvāhya maṇḍale devam kṛtvā nyāsam tu tasya ca mudrām pradarśya pādya dīn dadyāt mūlena śaṅkara dīpam naivedyam ācāmam namaskāram pradakṣiṇam nara pūjyāya kīrtyāya stutyāya vara dāya ca saṃkāra vṛkṣa chetre ca māyā bhetre namaḥ namaḥ vahu rūpāya tīrthāya triguṇāya aguṇāya ca sarvagam viṣṇum gataḥ aham śaraṇam gataḥ jñāna dīpa pradānena tamaḥ muktam prakāśaya stātraiḥ stutvā vai nīlalohita pañcatattva samāyuktam dhyāyot viṣṇum naraḥ hṛdi sarva kāma pradā śreṣṭhā vāsudevasya śaṅkara idam ca yaḥ paṭhet rudra pañcatattva arcanam naraḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe ca navidhiḥ nāma dvātriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam sudarśanasya pūjām me vada śaṅkha gadā dhara graha roga ādikam sarvam yat kṛtvā nāśam eti vai sudarśanasya cakrasya śṛṇu pūjām vṛṣadhvaja mūlamantreṇa vai nyāsam mūlamantram śṛṇuṣva ca sahasrāram hum phaṭ namaḥ mantraḥ praṇava pūrvakaḥ kathitaḥ sarva duṣṭānām nāśakaḥ mantra bhedakaḥ dhyāyet mudarśanam devam hṛdi padme amale śubhe āvāhya maṇḍale devam pūrva ukta vidhinā hara pūjayitvā japet mantram śatam aṣṭa uttaram naraḥ jvālā mālā pradīptāya sahasra ārāya cakṣuṣe sarva duṣṭa vināśāya sarva pātaka mardine pālana arthāya lokānām duṣṭa asura vināśine ugrāya ca eva saumyāya caṇḍāya ca namaḥ namaḥ namaḥ cakṣuḥ kva rūpāya saṃsāra bhaya bhedine māyā pañjara bhetre ca śivāya ca namaḥ namaḥ graha ati graha rūpāya grahāṇām pateya namaḥ kālāya mṛtyave ca eva bhīmāya ca namaḥ namaḥ viṣṇu rūpāya śāntāya ca āyudhānām dharāya ca sa pāpam bhasmasātkṛtvā viṣṇu lokāya kalpate iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe sudarśanapūjāvidhiḥ nāma trayastriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam punar deva arcanam brūhi hṛṣīkeśa gadādhara tat śṛṇuṣva jagannāthaḥ yena viṣṇuḥ pratuṣyati pravakṣyāmi param puṇyam tat ādau śṛṇu śaṅkara om saiṃ kṣaiṃ śirase namaḥ iti praṇava saṃyutaḥ asya aṅgāni mahādeva tān śṛṇuṣva vṛṣadhvaja om kṣāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ om śirase svāhā śiraḥ proktam vaṣaṭ tathā om kāra yuktā devasya śikhā jñeyā vṛṣadhvaja om kṣaiṃ kavacāya hum vai kavacam parikīrtitam om netratrayāya vauṣaṭ netram devasya kīrtitam om haḥ astrāya phaṭ astram devasya kīrtitam pūjā vidhim pravakṣyāmi nat me nigadataḥ śṛṇu ādau snātvā tathā ācamya tatas yāga gṛham vrajet tatas praviśya vidhivat śoṣaṇa ādikam yaṃ kṣaiṃ ram iti bījaiḥ ca kaṭhinīkṛtya lam iti aṇḍam utpādya ca tatas om kāreṇa eva bhedayet aṇḍa madhye hayagrīvam ātmānam paricintayet go kṣīra sadṛśam tadvat sūrya koṭi samagra bham su cakram su kapolam ca ṣīta ambara dharam vibhum tatas ca darśayet mudrām śaṅkhapadma ādikām śubhām dhyāyet dhyātvā arcayet viṣṇum mūlamantreṇa śaṅkara tatas ca āvāhayet rudra devatāḥ āsanasya yāḥ om hayagrīva āsanasya āgacchata ca devatāḥ āvāhya maṇḍale tāḥ tu pūjayet svastika ādike dvāre dhātuḥ vidhātuḥ ca pūjā kāryā vṛṣadhvaja asya madhyeḥ acanam kāryam dvāre gaṅgām ca pūjayet yamunām ca mahā devīm śaṅkha padma nidhī tathā garuḍam pūjayet agre madhye śaktim ca pūjayet ādhāra ākhyām mahādeva tatas kūrmam samarcayet anantam pṛthivīm paścāt dharma jñāne tatas vairāgyam atha ca aiśvaryam āgneya ādiṣu pūjayet adharma ajñāna avairāgya anaiśrargya ādīn tu pūrvatas arka soma agni saṃjñānām maṇḍalānām hi pūjanam pūrva ādiṣu ca patreṣu pūjyāḥ ca vimalā ādayaḥ anugrahā karṇikāyām pūjyā śreyaḥ arthibhiḥ naraiḥ snāna gandha pradānena puṣpa dhūpa pradānataḥ dīpa naivedya dānena āsanasya arcanam śubham vāma nāsā puṭena eva āgacchantam vicintayet āgacchataḥ prayogeṇa mūlamantreṇa śaṅkara āvāhya maṇḍale tasya nyāsam kuryāt atandritaḥ indra ādi lokapālaiḥ ca saṃyuktam viṣṇum avyayam pradarśayet mudrāḥ śaṅkha cakra ādikāḥ śubhāḥ devam saṃsthāpya vidhivat vastram dadyāt vṛṣadhvaja tatas hi ācamanam dadyāt upavītam tatas śubham dhyātvā pādya ādikam bhūyas dadyāt devāya śaṅkara dadyāt bhairava devāya mūlamantreṇa śaṅkara om kṣīṃ śirase namaḥ ca śirasaḥ pūjanam bhavet om kṣūṃ śikhāyai namaḥ ca śikhām etena pūjayet om kṣaiṃ kavacāya namaḥ kavacam paripūjayet om kṣaiṃ netrāya namaḥ ca netram ca anena pūjayet om kṣaḥ astrāya namaḥ iti astram ca anena pūjayet pūrva ādiṣu pradeśeṣu hi etāḥ tu paripūjayet pūjayet paramām devīm lakṣmīm lakṣmī pradām śubhām śaṅkham padmam tathā cakram gadām pūrva āditaḥ racayet khaḍgam ca musalam pāśam aṅkuśam sa śaram dhanuḥ brahmāṇam nāradam siddham gurum para gurum tathā indram sa vāhanam ca atha parivāra yutam tathā agnim yamam nirṛtim ca varuṇam vāyum eva ca somam īśānam evam vai brahmāṇam paripūjayet triśūlam cakra padme ca āyudhāni atha pūjayet viṣvaksenam tatas devam aiśānyām diśi pūjayet pūjā kāryā mahādeva hi anantasya vṛṣadhvaja devasya mūlamantreṇa pūjā kāryā vṛṣadhvaja stuvīta ca anyā stutyā praṇava ādyaiḥ vṛṣadhvaja om namaḥ haya śirase vidyā adhyakṣāya vai namaḥ namaḥ śāntāya devāya triguṇāya ātmane namaḥ namaḥ ca īśvara vandyāya śaṅka cakra dhāraya ca triguṇāya aguṇāya eva brahma viṣṇu svarūpiṇe iti evam saṃstavam kṛtvā devadevam vicintayet iti te kathitā pūjā hayagrīvasya śaṅkara iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe hayagrīvapūjāvidhiḥ nāma catustriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam nyāsa ādikam pravakṣyāmi gāyattryāḥ śṛṇu śaṅkara viśvāmitra ṛṣiḥ ca eva savitā ca atha devatā brahma śīrṣā rudra śikhā viṣṇoḥ hṛdaya saṃśritā evam jñātvā tu gāyattrīm japet dvādaśa lakṣakam tripadā aṣṭa akṣarā jñeyā catuṣpādā ṣaṣ akṣarā gāyattrīm vinyaset nityam sarva pāpa graṇa āśinīm gulpha madhye jaṅghayoḥ viddhi jānunoḥ ūrvoḥ guhye ca vṛṣaṇe nāḍyām nābhau tanū udare stanayoḥ hṛdi kaṇṭha oṣṭha mukhe tāluni ca aṃsayoḥ netre bhuvār lalāṭe ca pūrvasyām dakṣiṇa uttare paścame mūrdhni ca ākāram nyaset varṇān vadāmi aham indranīlam ca vahnim ca pītam śyāmam ca kāpilam śvetam vidyutprabham tāram kṛṣṇam raktam krameṇa tat śyāmam śuklam tathā pītam śvetam vai padmarāga vat śaṅkha varṇam pāṇḍuram ca raktam ca āsava sannibham arka varṇa samam saumyam śaṅkha ābham śvetam eva ca yat yat spṛśati hastena yat ca paśyati cakṣuṣā pūtam bhavati tat sarvam gāyattryāḥ na param viduḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam manaḥ vāk rāya jam doṣam prāṇāyāmaiḥ dahet dvijaḥ sāyam agniḥ ca mā iti uktā prātar sūrya iti apaḥ pibet āpohiṣṭhā iti ṛcā kuryāt mārjanam tu kuśa udakaiḥ vāc manaḥ karma jān doṣān nava etān navabhiḥ dahet samuddhṛtya udakam pāṇau japtvā ca drupadām kṣipet tri paṭ aṣṭau dvādaśadhā vartayet agham arpaṇam divā rātrau ca yat pāpam sarvam naśyati tad kṣaṇāt pūrva saṃdhyām japan tiṣṭhet paścimām upaviśya ca mahāvyāhṛti saṃyuktām gāyattrīm praṇava anvitām tri yugam tu sahasreṇa gāyattrī hanti duṣkṛtam raktā bhavati gāyattrī sāvitrī śukla varṇikā kṛṣṇā sarasvatī jñeyā saṃdhyā trayam udāhṛtam om bhūḥ vinyasya hṛdaye om bhuvaḥ śirasi nyaset om svar iti śikhāyām ca gāyattryāḥ prathamam padam tṛtīyena aṅga vinyāsam caturtham sarvatas nyaset śivaḥ tasyāḥ tu sarva ahne prāṇāyāma param nyaset viniyogam ṛṣicchandaḥ jñātvā tu japam ārabhet iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe saṃdhyāvidhiḥ nāma ṣaṭtriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam gāyattrī paramā devī bhukti mukti pradā ca tām saṃdhyāyām sarva pāpa ghnīm devīm āvāhya pūjayet bhūḥ bhuvaḥ svar sva mantreṇa yutām dvādaśa nāmabhiḥ vedamātre ca sāṃkṛtyai brahmāṇī kauśikī kramāt yathā lakṣam tu japtavyam uttare śikhare jātā bhūmyām parvata vāsinī brahmaṇā samanujñātā gaccha devi yathāsukham iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe gāyattrīkalpanirūpaṇam nāma saptatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam navamī ādau yajet durgām hrīṃ durge rakṣiṇi iti ca mārge tṛtīyām ārabhya pūjayet na viyoga bhāj dhanuḥ dhvajam ḍamarukam paraśum pāśam eva ca kapāla śaraka aṅkuśān brahmāṇi ehi ehi māheśvari ehi ehi kaumāri ehi ehi vārāhi ehi ehi aṣṭa uttara padānām hi mālā mantra mayī japaḥ ekaikra padam aṣṭa sahasradhā tri madhura akta tila aṣṭa sahasra hā ameḥ tilān trimadhura aktān ca sahasram ca aṣṭa homayet mahāmāṃsam trimadhurāt atha vā sarva karma kṛt vāri sarṣapa bhasma ādi kṣepāt yuddha ādike jayaḥ aṣṭāviṃśa bhujā dhyeyā aṣṭādaśa bhujā athavā dvādaśa aṣṭa kila bhūta īśe sarva bhūta samāvṛte rakṣa mām nija bhūtebhyaḥ valim gṛhṇa namaḥ astu te iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe durgājapapūjābalimantranirūpaṇam nāma sūtram kārpāsikam kramāt jñeyam kuja ādau saṃgrāhyam kanyayā kartitam ca yat dhūpayet īśamantreṇa tantudevāḥ iti (me smṛtāḥ om kāraḥ candramāḥ vahniḥ brahnā nāgaḥ śikhidhvajaḥ raviḥ viṣṇuḥ śivaḥ proktaḥ kramāt tantuṣu devatāḥ aṣṭottaraśatam kuryāt pañcāśat pañcaviṃśatim rudraḥ attamādi vijñeyam mānam ca granthayaḥ daśa catur aṅgula antarāḥ syuḥ granthi nāmāni ca kramāt manonmanī sarvamukhī dvi aṅgula aṅgulataḥ athavā saptamyām vā trayodaśyām śukla pakṣe tathā itare kṣīra ādibhiḥ ca saṃsnāpya liṅgam gandha ādibhiḥ yajet puṣpam gandha yutam dadyāt mūlena īśāna gocare pūrve ca daṇḍa dattvā dadyāt bhūta balim tathā āmantritaḥ asi deveśa gaṇaiḥ sārdham maheśvara nimantrya anena tiṣṭhet tu kurvan gīta ādikam niśi mantritāni pavitrāṇi sthāpayet deva pārśvataḥ snātvā ādityam tattva ātmakam paścāt devaka ākhyam tatas racayet om hauṃ hauṃ śivatattvāya namaḥ om hīṃ vidyā tattvāya namaḥ om hāṃ (hauḥ ātma tattvāya namaḥ om hāṃ hīṃ sarva tattvāya namaḥ pūraya pūraya makha diśet balim dattvā dvijān bhojya caṇḍam prācyai visarjayet iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe śivapavitrāropaṇam nāma dvicatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam purā devāsure yuddhe brahma ādyāḥ śaraṇam yayuḥ viṣṇuḥ ca teṣām devānām dhvajam graiveyakam dadau etau dṛṣṭvā vinaṅkṣyanti dānavān abravīt hariḥ viṣṇu ukte hi abravīt nāgaḥ vāsukeḥ anujaḥ tadā vṛṇīta ca vapitṛ ākhyam varam ca idam vṛṣadhvaja prāvṛṣ kāle tu ye martyāḥ na arciṣyanti pavitrakaiḥ teṣām sāṃvatsarī pūjā viphalā ca bhaviṣyati tasmāt sarveṣu deveṣu pavitra āropaṇam kramāt dvādaśyām pavitram tu pratimāyām ca kārayet ca jānubhyām avalambinī ṣaṭtriṃśat ca caturviśat dvādaśa granthayaḥ athavā uttama ādiṣu vijñeyāḥ parvabhiḥ vā pavitrakam aśvattha patra puṭake aṣṭa dikṣu niveśitam candanam nīla yuktam ca tila bhasma akṣatam tathā āgneya ādiṣu koṇa iṣuḥ śyā ādīnām tu kramāt nyaset dṛṣṭvā punar prapūjya atha vastreṇa ācchādya yatnataḥ devasya puratas sthāpyam pratimā maṇḍalasya vā brāhma ādīn ca api saṃsthāpya kalaśam ca api pūjayet astreṇa maṇḍalam kṛtvā naivedyam ca samarpayet vedikām veṣṭayitvā tu kalaśam ghṛtam agnikuṇḍam vimānam ca maṇḍapam gṛham eva ca sūtram ekam tu saṃgṛhya dadyāt devasya āvāhitaḥ asi deveśa pūjā artham parameśvara gāyattryā ca arcitam tena devam saṃpūjya dāpayet agnim saṃtarpya tatra api dvādaśa aṅgula mānataḥ devasya agre paṭhet mantram kṛtāñjali puṭaḥ sthitaḥ tat sarvam pūrṇam eva astu tvad prasādāt sureśvara vanamālā yathā deva kaustubham satatam hṛdi iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe viṣṇupavitrāropaṇam nāma tricatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam jñānam mahati saṃyacchet yaḥ icchet jñānam ātmāni varjitam bhūta tanmātraiḥ guṇa janma aśana ādibhiḥ sva prakāśam nirākāram sa dānam damana ādi yat ahiṃsā ādiḥ yamaḥ proktaḥ śauca ādiḥ niyamaḥ smṛtaḥ āsanam padmaka ādi uktam prāṇāyāmaḥ marut jayaḥ pūrvam cetaḥ sthiram na syāt tatas mūrtim vicintayet hṛd padma karṇikā madhye śaṅkha cakra gadā abjavān nityaḥ śuddhaḥ bhūti yuktaḥ satya ānanda āhvayaḥ paraḥ caturviśati mūrtiḥ sa śālagrāma śilā sthitaḥ dvārakā ādi śilā saṃsthaḥ dhyeyaḥ pūjyaḥ api aham ca saḥ niṣkāmaḥ muktim āpnoti mūrtim iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe brahmamūrtidhyānanirūpaṇam nāma catuścatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam hṛṣīkeśāya abja gadā śaṅkhine cakriṇe namaḥ su śaṅkha su gadā abja ari dhṛte sa śaṅka cakra abja gadam sa gadaḥ sa ariḥ padma śaṅkhin namaḥ namaḥ su cakra abja gadā śaṅkha yuktāya hari mūrtaye pṛthu vakṣāḥ vaḥ kapilaḥ varāhaḥ śakti liṅgaḥ pṛthu chidraḥ sthūla cakraḥ kṛṣṇaḥ viṣṇuḥ ca bilva vat vaikuṇṭhaḥ maṇi ratna ābhaḥ eka cakra ambujaḥ asitaḥ svarṇa rekhā samāyuktaḥ goṣpadena virājitaḥ kadamba kusuma ākāraḥ lakṣmīnārāyaṇaḥ avatu viṣṇoḥ mūrti mayam stotram yaḥ paṭhet sa divam vrajet iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe śālagrāmamūrtilakṣaṇam nāma pañcacatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam vāstum saṃkṣepataḥ vakṣye gṛha ādau vighna nāśanam īsāna koṇāt ārabhya hi ekāśīti pade yajet īśāne ca śiraḥ pādau nairṛte agni anile karau āvāsa vāsa veśma ādau pure grāme vaṇikpathe prāsāda ārāma durgeṣu devālaya maṭheṣu ca dvāviṃśati surān bāhye tad antar ca trayodaśa bahis dvātriṃśat ete tu tad antar caturaḥ śṛṇu devān eka uttarān etān pūrva ādau nāmataḥ śṛṇu īśāna koṇāt ārabhya durge car vaṃśaḥ ucyate āgneya koṇāt ārabhya vaṃśaḥ bhavati durdharaḥ nāyikā kālikā nāma punar vāstu devān pūjayitvā gṛha prāsāda kṛt bhavet evam viṣṇu āśramam kuryāt vanaiḥ ca upavanaiḥ yutam karṇe ca eva atha śikhi ādyāḥ tathā devāḥ prakīrtitāḥ tebhyaḥ hi ubhayatas sārdhāt anye api dvipadāḥ surāḥ catuḥ ṣaṣṭi padāḥ devāḥ iti evam parikīrtitāḥ carakī ca vidārī ca pūtanā pāpa rākṣasī īśāna ādyāḥ tatas bāhye deva ādyāḥ hetuka ādayaḥ agnijihvaḥ kālakaḥ ca karālaḥ aiśānyām bhīma rūpaḥ tu pātāle preta nāyakaḥ ākāśe gandhamālī syāt kṣetrapālān tatas yajet vistāra abhihatam dairghyam rāśim vāstoḥ tu kārayet kṛtvā ca vasubhiḥ bhāgam śeṣam baddhā yam ādiśet punar guṇitam aṣṭābhiḥ ṛ bhāgam tu bhājayet yat śeṣam tat bhavet ṛkṣam bhāgaiḥ hṛtvā avyayam bhavet ṛkṣam caturguṇam kṛtvā navabhiḥ bhāga hāritam śeṣam aṃśam vijānīyāt devalasya matam yathā aṣṭābhiḥ guṇitam piṇḍam ṣaṣṭibhiḥ bhāgāharitam yat śeṣam tat bhavet jīvam maraṇam vāstu kroḍe gṛham kuryāt na pṛṣṭhe mānavaḥ sadā siṃha kanyā tulāyām ca dvāram śudhyet atha uttaram evam ca vṛścika ādau syāt pūrva dakṣiṇa paścimam dvāram dīrgha ardha vistāram dvārāṇi aṣṭau smṛtāni ca santāna preṣya nīca tvam sva yānam svarṇa bhūṣaṇam suta hīnam tu raudreṇa vīrya ghnam dakṣiṇe tathā vahnau badhaḥ ca āyuḥ vṛddhim puttra lābha su tṛpti daḥ dhanade nṛpa pīḍā dam artha ghnam roga dam jale dvārāṇi uttara saṃjñāni pūrva dvārāṇi vacmi aham rāja ghnam kopa dam pūrve phalataḥ dvāram īritam īśāna ādau bhavet pūrvam agneyī ādau tu dakṣiṇam nairṛtya ādau paścimam syāt vāyavī ādau tu ca uttaram aṣṭa bhāge kṛte bhāge dvārāṇām ca phala aphalam aśvattha plakṣa nyagrodhāḥ pūrva ādau syāt udumbaraḥ gṛhasya śobhanaḥ proktaḥ īśāne ca eva sālmaliḥ pūjitaḥ vigna hārī syāt prāsādasya gṛhasya ca iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe vāstumānalakṣaṇam nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam sūtaḥ uvāca prāsādānām lakṣaṇam ca vakṣye śaunaka tat śṛṇu catuḥ ṣaṣṭi padam kṛtvā diś vidikṣu upalakṣitam catuṣkoṇam caturbhiḥ ca dvārāṇi sūrya saṃkhyayā bhittīnām kalpanā bhavet ūrdhva kṣetra samā jaṅghā jaṅghā ardha dviguṇam bhavet garbha vistāra vistīrṇaḥ śuka aṅghriḥ ca vidhīyate nirgamaḥ tu śuka aṅghreḥ ca ucchrāyaḥ śikhara ardha gaḥ liṅga mānam atho vakṣye pīṭhaḥ liṅga samaḥ bhavet dviguṇam śikharam proktam jaṅghāyāḥ ca eva śaunaka pīṭha garbha avaram karma tad mānena śuka aṅghrikam niḥ gamaḥ tu samākhyātaḥ śeṣam pūrvavat eva tu liṅga mānam smṛtam hi etat dvāra mānam atha ucyate kara agram veda vat kṛtvā dvāram bhāga aṣṭamam bhavet vistareṇa samākhyātam dviguṇam sva icchayā bhavet dvāra vat pīṭha madhye tu śeṣam suṣirakam bhavet pādikam śeṣikam bhittiḥ dvāra ardhena parigrahāt tad vistāra samā jaṅghā sikharam dviguṇam bhavet śuka aṅghriḥ pūrva vat jñeyā nirgama ucchrāyakam bhavet maṇḍape mānam etat tu svarūpam ca aparam vade garbham tu dviguṇam kuryāt nemyāḥ mānam bhavet iha prāsādānām ca vakṣyāmi mānam yonim ca mānataḥ triviṣṭapam ca pañca ete prāsādāḥ sarva yonayaḥ etebhyaḥ eva sambhūtāḥ prāsādāḥ su manoharāḥ meruḥ ca mandaraḥ ca eva vimānaḥ ca tathā aparaḥ nandivardhana saṃjñaḥ ca śrīvatsaḥ ca navā iti amī caturaśrāḥ samudbhūtāḥ vairājāt iti gamyatām vajram cakram tathā anyat ca muṣṭikam vabhru saṃjñitam vijayaḥ nāmataḥ śvetaḥ triviṣṭipa samudbhavāḥ kuryāt haja ādikam khyāta dvāri garbhagṛham tathā maṇāḍapaḥ sama saṃkhyābhiḥ guṇitaḥ sūtrakaḥ tathā maṇḍapasya caturtha aṃśāt bhadraḥ kāryaḥ vijānatā sārdha bhitti pramāṇena bhiti mānena vā punar bhitteḥ dvaiguṇyataḥ vā api kartavyāḥ maṇḍapāḥ kracid prāsāde mañcarī kāryā citrā viṣama bhūmikā parimāṇa virodhena rekhā vaiṣamya bhūṣitā ādhāraḥ tu catur dhāraḥ catur maṇḍapa śobhitaḥ śata śṛṅga samāyuktaḥ meruḥ prāsādaḥ uttamaḥ maṇḍapāḥ tasya kartavyāḥ bhadraiḥ tribhiḥ alaṅkṛtāḥ gha cana akāra mānānām bhinna abhinnāḥ bhavanti te kiyantaḥ yeṣu ca ādhārāḥ nirādhārāḥ ca kecana anyonya asaṃkarāḥ teṣām ghaṭanānām abhedataḥ devatānām viśeṣāya prāsādāḥ bahavaḥ smṛtāḥ prāsāde niyamaḥ na asti devatānām svayambhuvām tān eva devatānām ca pūrva mānena kārayet caturaśra āyatāḥ tattra catuṣkoṇa samanvitāḥ candraśālā anvitā kāryā bherī śikhara saṃyutā puratas vāhanānām ca kartavyāḥ lagna maṇḍapāḥ nāṭya śālā ca kartavyā dvāra deśa samāśrayā prasāde devatānām ca kāryā dikṣu vidikṣu api dvārapālāḥ ca kartavyāḥ mukhyāḥ gatvā pṛthak pṛthak kiñcid adūrataḥ kāryāḥ maṭhāḥ tatra upajīvinām prāvṛtā jagatī kāryā phala puṣpa jala anvitā prasādeṣu surān sthāpya pūjābhiḥ pūjayet naraḥ vāsudevaḥ sarva devaḥ sarva bhāj tad gṛha ādi kṛt iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśe ācāra kāṇḍe prāsādaliṅgamaṇḍapādilakṣaṇanirūpaṇam nāma saptacatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam sūtaḥ uvāca pratiṣṭhām sarva devānām saṃkṣepeṇa vadāmi aham su tithi ādau su ramyām ca pratiṣṭhām kārayet guruḥ mantra nyāsam guruḥ kṛtvā tatas karma samārabhet kuryāt dvādaśa hastam vā stambhaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ yutam dhvaja aṣṭakaiḥ catur hastām madhye vedim ca kārayet vālukām tatra dāpayet caturaśram kārmuka ābham vartulam kamala ākṛti pūrva āditaḥ samārabhya kartavyam kuṇḍa pañcakam athavā caturaśrāṇi sarvāṇi etāni kārayet śiraḥ sthāne tu devasya ācāryaḥ homam ācaret aiśānyām kecid icchanti upalipya avanim śubhām dvārāṇi ca eva catvāri kṛtvā vai toraṇa antike nyagrodha udumbara aśvattha bailva pālāśa khādirāḥ toraṇāḥ pañca hastāḥ ca vastra puṣpa ādi alaṅkṛtāḥ pūrva dvāre mṛga indram tu haya rājam tu dakṣiṇe mantreṇa dakṣiṇasyām dvitīyakam pūrve ambuda vat kāryā āgnoyyām dhūma rūpiṇī yāmyām vai kṛṣṇa rūpā tu śyāmalā bhavet vāruṇyām pāṇḍurā jñeyā vāyavyām pīta varṇikā uttare rakta varṇā tu śukla īśī ca patākikā ā agnim saṃsupti mantreṇa yamaḥ nāga iti dakṣiṇe tam īśānam atas ca eva viṣṇoḥ nukā iti madhyame vastra yugma samāyuktāḥ candana ādyaiḥ su alaṅkṛtāḥ puṣpaiḥ vitānaiḥ bahulaiḥ ādi varṇa abhimantritāḥ dikpālāḥ ca tatas pūjyāḥ śāstra dṛṣṭena karmaṇā asmin vṛkṣe ca eva pracārī iti parā smṛtā kiñca id adhātu ca saptamī imāḥ rudra iti dikyālān pūjayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ homa dravyāṇi vāyavye kuryāt sa upaskarāṇi ca śaṅkhān śāstra uditān śvetān netrābhyām vinyaset guruḥ ālokanena dravyāṇi śuddhim yānti na saṃśayaḥ tdṛdaya ādīni ca aṅgāni vyāhṛti praṇavena ca astram ca eva samastānām nyāsaḥ ayam sarvakāmikaḥ akṣatān viṣṭaram ca eva astreṇa eva abhimantritān viṣṭareṇa spṛśet duvyān yāga maṇḍapa saṃbhṛtān akṣatān vikiret paścāt astra pūtān samantataḥ avakīrya akṣatārn saṃvān lepayet maṇḍapam tatas pratiṣṭhā yasya devasya tad ākhyam kalaśam nyaset aiśānyām pūjayet yāmye astreṇa eva ca bardhanīm kalaśam vardhanīm ca eva grahān vāsttoṣpatim tathā āsana itāni sarvāṇi praṇava ākhyam japet guruḥ sarva oṣadhī gandha liptam pūjayet kalaśam guruḥ vardhanyā tu samāyuktam kalaśam bhrāmayet anu ghaṭam ca āvāhya vāyavyām gaṇānām tvā iti sat gaṇam devam īśāna koṇe tu japet vāstoṣpatim budhaḥ vāstoṣpatī iti mantreṇa vāstu doṣa upaśāntaye paṭhet iti ca vidyāḥ ca kuryāt ālambhanam budhaḥ yoge yoga iti mantreṇa āstaraṇam śādvalaiḥ kuśaiḥ ṛtvigbhiḥ sārdham ācāryaḥ snāna pīṭhe guruḥ tadā kṛtvā brahma rathe devam pratiṣṭhanti tatas dvijāḥ aiśānyām ānayet pīṭha maṇḍape vinyaset guruḥ saṃsnāpya lakṣaṇa uddhāram kuryāt tūrya ādi dūra abhi vādanaiḥ akṣiṇī ca añjayet ca asya suvarṇasya śalākayā agniḥ jyotiḥ iti mantreṇa tu kārayet lakṣaṇe kriyamāṇe tu nāma ekam sthāpakaḥ imaṃmegaṅgemantreṇa netrayoḥ śītala kriyā bilva udumbaram aśvattham vaṭam pālāśam eva ca sahadevī balā ca eva śatamūlī śatāvarī kumārī ca guḍūcī ca siṃhī vyāghrī tathā eva ca yāḥ phalinī iti mantreṇa phala snānam vidhīyate ratnāni ca eva dhānyāni oṣadhīm śatapuṣpikām samudrān ca eva vinyasya caturaḥ caturaḥ diśaḥ kṣīram dadhi kṣīrodasya ghṛtodasya iti vā punar āpyāyasva dadhikrāvṇaḥ yāḥ auṣadhīḥ iti iti ca samudra ākhyaiḥ caturbhiḥ ca snāpayet kalaśaiḥ punar snātaḥ ca eva su veṣaḥ ca dhūpaḥ deyaḥ ca gugguluḥ mantreṇa ānayet maṇḍapam śubham vastreṇa ācchādayitvā tu pūjanīyaḥ svabhāvataḥ yathāśāstram nivedyāni pāda mūle tu dāpayet kalaśam sa hiraṇyam ca śiraḥ sthāne nivedayet sthitvā kuṇḍa samīpe atha agneḥ sthāpanam ācaret śrīsūktam pāvamānyam ca vā āsadāmi a sa vājinam vṛṣākapim ca mitram bahu a caḥ pūrvatas japet brahmāṇam pitṛ maitram ca adhvaryuḥ dakṣiṇe japet kuṇḍam ca astreṇa saṃprokṣya ācāryaḥ tu viśeṣataḥ tāmra pātre śarāve vā yathā vibhavataḥ api vā amṛtīkṛtya tam paścāt mantraiḥ sarvaiḥ ca deśikaḥ pātram gṛhya karābhyām ca kuṇḍam bhrāmya tatas punar dakṣiṇe sthāpayet brahma praṇītān ca uttareṇa tu sādhāraṇena mantreṇa sva sūtra vihitena vā dikṣu dikṣu tatas dadyāt paridhim viṣṭaraiḥ saha brahma viṣṇu hara īśānāḥ pūjyāḥ sādhāraṇena tu pavitram tu tatas kṛtvā kuryāt ājyasya saṃskṛtim ācāryaḥ atha nirīkṣya api nīrājyam abhimantritam ājyabhāga abhighāra antam avekṣeta ājya siddhaye pañca pañca āhutīḥ hutvā ājyena tad anantaram garbhādhāna āditas tāvat yāvat gaudānikam bhavet tatas pūrṇāhutim dattvā pūrṇāt pūrṇam an ārethaḥ pūjayitvā tatas vahnim kuṇḍeṣu viharet tathā indra ādīnām sva mantraiḥ ca tathā āhuti śatam śatam purṇāhutim śatasya ante sarveṣām ca eva homayet svām āhutim atha ājyeṣu hotā tad kalaśe nyaset niṣkṛṣya bahis ācāryaḥ dikpālānām balim haret bhūtānām ca eva devānām nāgānām ca prayogataḥ ājyam tayoḥ sahakāri tat pradhānam yat aṅka (kṣa yoḥ jyeṣṭhasāma ca bhāruṇḍam tat nayāmi iti paścime evam madhye tathā pāde pūrṇāhutyā tathā punar śiraḥ sthāneṣu juhuyāt āviśet ca api anukramāt vedānām ādi mantraiḥ vā mantraiḥ vā deva nāmabhiḥ sva śāstra vihitaiḥ vā api gāyattryā vā atha te dvijāḥ gāyattryā vā athavā ācāryaḥ vyāhṛti praṇavena tu evam homa vidhim kṛtvā nyaset mantrān tu deśikaḥ caraṇau agnim īḍe tu gulphayoḥ sthitāḥ agne āyāhi jaṅghe dve śannodevī iti jānunī dīrgha āyuḥ tvāya hṛdaye śrīḥ ca te galake nyaset tathā mūrdhni ālagnāt homam ācaret veda puṇya aha śabdena prāsādānām pradakṣiṇam piṇḍikā ālaṃbhanam kṛtvā devasya tvā iti mantra vid dikpāḥ lān saha ratnaiḥ ca dhātūn oṣadhayaḥ tathā lauha bījāni siddhāni paścāt devam tu vinyaset īṣat madhyam parityajya tatas doṣa apaham tu tat tilasya tuṣa mātram tu uttaram kiñcid ānayet tattva varṇa kalā mātram prajāni bhuvana ātmaje pātram vastra yugam chatram tathā divya aṅgulīyakam ṛttvigbhyaḥ ca pradātavyā dakṣiṇā ca eva śaktitas caturthau juhuyāt paścāt yajamānaḥ samāhitaḥ āhutīnām śatam hutvā tatas pūrṇām pradāpayet niṣkramya bahis ācāryaḥ dikpālānām balim haret yāga ante kapilām dadyāt ācāryāya ca cāmaram mukuṭam kuṇḍalam chatram keyūram kaṭi sūtrakam vyajanam grāma vastra ādīn sa upaskāram su maṇḍapam bhojanam ca mahāt kuryāt kṛtakṛtyaḥ ca jāyate iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe devapratiṣṭhādinirūpaṇam nāma aṣṭacatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam sarga ādi kṛt hariḥ ca eva pūjyaḥ svāyambhuva ādibhiḥ vipra ādyaiḥ svena dharmeṇa tad dharmam vyāsa vai śṛṇu kṣamā damaḥ dayā dānam alobhāḥ abhyāsaḥ eva ca ete āśramikāḥ dharmāḥ caturvarṇyam bavīmi atas ānandam aiśvaram yasmāt muktaḥ na āvartate naraḥ muktiḥ aṣṭa aṅga vijñānāt saṃkṣepāt tat vade śṛṇu yamāḥ pañca tu ahiṃsā ādyāḥ ahiṃsā prāṇi ahiṃsanam satyam bhūta hitam vākyam asteyam sva agraham param niyamāḥ pañca satya ādyāḥ bāhmam ābhyantaram dvidhā śaucam tuṣṭiḥ ca santoṣaḥ tapaḥ ca indriya nigrahaḥ svādhyāyaḥ syāt mantra jāpaḥ praṇidhānam hareḥ yajiḥ āsanam padmaka ādi uktam prāṇāyāmaḥ marut jayaḥ evam dvidhā tridhā api uktam puraṇāt pūrakaḥ sa ca ṣaṭtriṃśat mātrikaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ pratyāhāraḥ ca rodhanam brahma ātma cintā dhyānam syāt dhāraṇā manasaḥ dhṛtiḥ aham brahma iti avasthānam samādhiḥ brahmaṇaḥ sthitiḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe varṇāśramadharmanirūpaṇam nāma ekonapañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam ahani ahani yaḥ kuryāt kriyām sa jñānam āpnuyāt sukhāt suptasya satatam lālā ādyāḥ saṃstravanti hi dhyātvā raktām sitām kṛṣṇām gāyattrīm vai japet vudhaḥ mantraiḥ tu vividhaiḥ sauraiḥ ṛk yajuḥ sāma saṃjñitaiḥ kurvīta praṇatim bhūmau mūrdhānam abhimantritaḥ sādhayet vividhān arthān kuṭumba artham tatas dvijaḥ pañca piṇḍān an uddhṛtya snānam duṣyanti nityaśas abhimantrya jalam mantraiḥ āliṅgaiḥ vāruṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ tnāna kāle smaret viṣṇamā apaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ yatas prekṣya oṅkāram ādityam tris nimajjet jalāśaye mantrān ca vividhān paścāt prāc kūle ca pitṝn devān munīn bhaktyā sva sūtra ukta vidhānataḥ deva ṛṣoṃ tarpayet dhīmān udaka añjalibhiḥ bhūta yajñaḥ sa vai jñeyaḥ bhūtebhyaḥ yaḥ tu ayam baliḥ ekam tu bhojayet vipram pitṝn uddiśya sattamāḥ aśaucam saṃpravakṣyāmi aśuciḥ pātakī sadā aśaucam ca eva saṃsargāt śuddhiḥ saṃsarga varjanāt śudhyet māsena vai śūdraḥ yatīnām na asti pātakam iti gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe nityakarmāśaucayornirūpaṇam nāma pañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam kusīdam kṛṣi vāṇijyam kṣattra vṛttaḥ atha varjayet an uddiśya phalam tasmāt brāhmaṇāya tu nityaśas upoṣya abhyarcayet vidvān madhunā tila sarpiṣā dvādaśyām arcayet viṣṇum upoṣya agha praṇāśanam brāhmaṇān pūjayet datnāt bhojayet yoṣitaḥ surān akāmaḥ sarva kāmaḥ vā pūjayet tu gadādharam iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe dānadharmanirūpaṇam nāma ekapañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam upapāpāni gohatyā prabhṛtīni surāḥ jaguḥ kapālamocane snātvā vārāṇasyām tathā eva ca suvarṇa steyī muktaḥ syāt musalena hataḥ nṛpaiḥ phalgutīrtha ādiṣu snātaḥ sarva ācāra phalam labhet iti āha bhagavān viṣṇuḥ purā mama yata vratāḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe prāyaścittanirūpaṇam nāma dvipañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam sūtaḥ uvāca evam brahmā abravīt śrutvā hareḥ aṣṭa nidhīn tathā mukunda kundau nīlaḥ ca śaṅkhaḥ ca eva aparaḥ nidhiḥ dākṣiṇya sāraḥ puruṣaḥ suvarṇa ādika saṃgraham mahāpadma aṅkitaḥ dadyāt dhana ādyam dhārmikāya ca nīdhī padma mahāpadmau sāttvikau puruṣau smṛtī makareṇa aṅkitaḥ khaḍga bāṇa kunta ādi saṃgrahī dadyāt śrutāya maitrīm ca yāti nityam ca rājabhiḥ dravya artham śatruṇā nāśam saṃgrāme ca api saṃvrajet nidhānam urvyām kurute nidhiḥ saḥ api eka pūruṣaḥ rājasena mukundena lakṣitā rājya saṃgrahī ca aṅkitaḥ sattva tejasā saṃyutaḥ bhavet vastra dhānya ādi saṃgrāhī taḍāga ādi karoti ca nidhiḥ ca eva āmra ārāma ādi kārayet ekasya syāt nidhiḥ śaṅkhaḥ svayam bhuṅkte dhana ādi sva poṣaṇa paraḥ śaṅkhī dadyāt para nare vṛthā miśra avalokanāt miśra svabhāva phala dāyinaḥ nidhīnām rūpam uktam tu hariṇā api hara ādike iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe navanidhivarṇanam nāma tripañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam jyotiṣmān daśamaḥ jātaḥ putrāḥ hi ete priyavratāt vibhajya sapta dvīpāni saptānām pradadau nṛpaḥ dvīpāt tu dviguṇaḥ dvīpaḥ samudraḥ ca vṛṣadhvaja jambūdvīpa īśvarāt putrāḥ hi agrīdhnāt abhavat nava ketumālaḥ nṛpaḥ tebhyaḥ tad saṃjñān khaṇḍakān dadau nābheḥ tu merudevyām tu putraḥ abhūt ṛṣabhaḥ hara indradyumnaḥ ca tad putraḥ parameṣṭhī tatas smṛtaḥ pratīhāraḥ ca tad putraḥ pratihartā tad ātmajaḥ iti śrā gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe bhuvanakośavarṇanopayogipriyavratavaṃśanirūpaṇam nāma catuḥ pañcaśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam varṣaḥ hiraṇvān vṛṣabhadhvaja tatas kimpuruṣaḥ varṣaḥ meroḥ dakṣiṇatas smṛtaḥ vedasmṛtiḥ narmadā ca varadā surasā śivā tāpī payoṣṇī sarayūḥ kāverī gomatī tathā ketumālā tāmraparṇā candrabhāgā sarasvatī ṛṣikulyā ca kāverī mattagaṅgā payasvinī vidarbhā ca śatadrūḥ ca nadyaḥ pāpa harāḥ śubhāḥ āsām pibanti salilam madhyadeśa ādayaḥ janāḥ pāñcālāḥ kuravaḥ matsyāḥ yaudheyāḥ sa paṭaccarāḥ kuntayaḥ śūrasenāḥ ca madhyadeśa janāḥ smṛtāḥ vṛṣadhvaja janāḥ pādmāḥ sūta māgadha cedayaḥ kāśayaḥ ca videhāḥ ca pūrvasyām kosalāḥ tathā kaliṅga vaṅga puṇḍra aṅgāḥ vaidarbhāḥ mūlakāḥ tathā vindhya antar nilayāḥ deśāḥ pūrva dakṣiṇatas smṛtāḥ pulanda aśmaka jīmūta naya rāṣṭra nivāsinaḥ dakṣiṇāpatha vāsinaḥ ambaṣṭha draviḍāḥ lāṭāḥ kāmbhojāḥ strīmukhāḥ śakāḥ ānarta vāsinaḥ ca eva jñeyāḥ yakṣiṇa paścime strī rājyāḥ saindhavāḥ mlecchāḥ na asti kāḥ yavanāḥ tathā paścimena ca vijñeyāḥ māthurāḥ naiṣadhaiḥ saha māṇḍavyāḥ ca tuṣārāḥ ca mūlika aśvamukhāḥ khaśāḥ mahākeśāḥ mahānāsāḥ deśāḥ tu uttara paścime lamba stana nāgāḥ ca mādra gāndhāra bāhlikāḥ himācala ālayāḥ mlecchāḥ udīcīm diśam āśritāḥ brahmaputrāḥ sa ṭaṅkaṇāḥ abhīṣāhāḥ sa kāśmīrāḥ udak parveṇa kīrtitāḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam vapuṣmān śālmalasya īśaḥ tad sutāḥ varṣa nāmakāḥ śākadvīpa īśvarāt bhavyāt sapta putrāḥ prajajñire śabalāt puṣkara īśāt ca mahāvīraḥ ca dhātakiḥ tamasā parvataḥ vyāptaḥ tamaḥ api aṇḍa kaṭāhataḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam raudre tu puṣkaradvīpe narakāḥ santi tān śṛṇu pāpinaḥ teṣu pacyante viṣa śastra agni dāyinaḥ upari upari vai lokāḥ rudra bhūta ādayaḥ sthitāḥ tat aṇḍam mahatā rudra pradhānena ca veṣṭitam iti gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe bhuvanakośagatāpātalanarakādinirūpaṇam nāma saptapañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam prahetiḥ kacchanīraḥ ca nāradaḥ ca eva mādhave hāhā rathasvanaḥ ca eva jyeṣṭhe bhānoḥ rathe sthitāḥ viśvācī senajit cāpaḥ kārtike ca adhikāriṇaḥ viśvāmitraḥ tathā rakṣaḥ yajñāpetaḥ hi phālgune sa upāsaṃga patākaḥ tu śukrasya api rathaḥ mahān sakṛt yaktāḥ tu bhūteśa bahanti aviratam śiva iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe bhuvanakośanirūpaṇam nāma aṣṭapañcāśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam atha jyotiḥ śāstram sūtaḥ uvāca jyotiḥ cakram bhuvaḥ mānam uktvā provāca keśavaḥ catur lakṣam jyotiṣasya sāram rudrāya sarva daḥ kṛttikāḥ tu agni devatyāḥ rohiṇyaḥ brahmaṇaḥ smṛtāḥ ilvalāḥ soma devatyāḥ raudram ca ārdram udāhṛtam aśleṣāḥ sarpa devatyāḥ maghāḥ ca pitṛ devatāḥ sāvitraḥ ca tathā hastā citrā tvaṣṭā prakīrtitaḥ svātī ca vāyu devatyā nakṣatram parikīrtitam indra agni devatā proktā viśākhā vṛṣabhadhvaja maitram ṛkṣam anūrādhā jyeṣṭhā śākram prakīrtitam tathā nirṛti devatyaḥ mūlaḥ tad jñaiḥ udāhṛtaḥ āpyāḥ tu āṣāṭhapūrvāḥ tu uttarāḥ vaiśvadevatāḥ brāhmaḥ ca eva abhijit proktaḥ śravaṇāḥ vaiṣṇavaḥ smṛtaḥ vāsavaḥ tu tathā ṛkṣam dhaniṣṭhā procyate budhaiḥ tathā śatabhiṣā proktam nakṣatram vāruṇam śiva ājam bhādrapadā pūrvā ahirbrudhnyaḥ tathā uttarā pauṣṇam ca revatī ṛkṣam aśvayuj ca aśva daivatam bharaṇī ṛkṣam tathā yāmyam proktāḥ te ṛkṣa devatāḥ brahmāṇī saṃsthitā pūrve pritapat navamī tithau māheśvarī ca uttare ca dvitīyā daśāmī tithau pañcamyām ca trayodaśyām vārāhī dakṣiṇe sthitā ṣaṣṭhyām ca eva caturdaśyām indrāṇī paścime sthitā saptamyām paurṇamāsyām ca cāmuṇḍāḥ vāyugocare aṣṭamī amāvāsyā yoge mahālakṣmīśa gocare ekādaśyām tṛtīyāyām agnikoṇe tu vaiṣṇavī dvādaśyām ca caturthyām tu kaumārī nairṛte tathā puṣyā hastā tathā jyeṣṭhā prasthāne śreṣṭham ucyate hasta ādi pañca ṛkṣāṇi uttarā trayam eva ca aśvinī rohiṇī puṣyā dhaniṣṭhā ca punarvasū vastra prāvaraṇe śreṣṭhaḥ nakṣatrāṇām gaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ kṛttikā bharaṇī aśleṣāḥ maghāḥ mūla viśākhayoḥ trīṇi pūrvā tathā ca eva adhovakrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ eṣu vāpī taḍāga ādi kūpa bhūmi tṛṇāni ca devāgārasya khananam nidhāna khananam tathā gaṇitam jyotiṣā ārambham khani bila praveśanam gaja uṣṭra aśva balīvarda damanam mahiṣasya ca bījānām vapanam kuryāt gamana āgamana ādikam cakra yantra rathānām ca nāvā ādīnām pravāhaṇam pārśveṣu yāni karmāṇi kuryāt eteṣu tāni api rohiṇī ārdrām tathā puṣyā dhaniṣṭhā ca uttarā trayam vāruṇam śravaṇam ca eva nava ca ūrdhva mukhāḥ smṛtāḥ ūrdhva mukhyāni ucchritāni sarvāṇi eteṣu kārayet ca aśubhā ṣaṣṭhī aṣṭamī navamī tathā amāvāsyā pūrṇimā ca tad vādaśī ca caturdaśī aśuklā pratipad śreṣṭhā dvitīyā candra sūnunā tṛtīyā bhūmiputreṇa caturthā ca śanaiścare gurau śubhā pañcamī syāt ṣaṣṭī maṅgala śukrayoḥ saptamī somaputreṇa aṣṭamī kuja bhāskarau navamī candra vāreṇa daśamī tu gurau śubhā ekādaśyāḥ guru śukrau dvādaśyām ca punar budhaḥ trayodaśī śukra bhaumau śanau śreṣṭhā caturdaśī paurṇamāsī api amāvāsyā śreṣṭhā syāt ca bṛhaspatau dvādaśīm dahate bhānuḥ śaśī ca ekādaśīm dahet kujaḥ dahet ca daśāmīm navamīm ca budhaḥ dahet aṣṭamīm dahate jīvaḥ saptamīm bhārgavaḥ dahet sūryaputraḥ dahet ṣaṣṭhīm gamana ādyāsu nā asti vai pratipad navamīṣu eva caturdaśī aṣṭamīṣu ca meṣe karkaṭake ṣaṣṭhī kanyāyām mithune aṣṭamī daśamī vṛścike siṃhe dhanuḥ mone caturdaśī etāḥ dagdhāḥ na gantavyam pīḍā ādiḥ kila mānavaiḥ viśākhā trayam āditye pūrvāṣāḍhā traye śaśī eṣu yogeṣu ca utpāta mṛtyu roga ādikam bhavet mūleḥ akaḥ śravaṇe candraḥ proṣṭhapadī uttare kujaḥ pūrvaphalgunī śukre ca svātiḥ ca eva śanaiśvare etaiḥ ca amṛta yogāḥ syuḥ sarva kārya prasādhakāḥ kālam parva ādiḥ tu jñeyaḥ kālaḥ kāla viśāradaiḥ ekīkṛtya akṣarāt mātram nāmnoḥ strīpuṃsayoḥ tribhiḥ bhāge dvi śeṣe strī nāśaḥ pusaḥ syāt eka śūnyayoḥ viṣkambhe ghaṭikāḥ pañca śūle sapta prakīrtitāḥ guruḥ puṣye anurādhā budhe śubhā rohiṇī ca śanau śreṣṭhā saumam somena vai śubham śukre ca revatī śreṣṭhā aśvinī maṅgale śubhā eteṣu siddhi yogāḥ vai sarva doṣa vināśanāḥ bhaume vā uttarāṣāḍhā dhaniṣṭhā ca budhe hara garau śatabhiṣā rudra śukre vai rohiṇī tathā śanau ca revatī śambho viṣa yogāḥ prakīrtitāḥ kuryāt śatabhiṣāyām ca jātakarma ādi mānavaḥ viśākhā ca uttarā trīṇi maghā ārdrā bharaṇī tathā āśleṣā kṛttikā rudra prasthāne maraṇa pradāḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe jyotiḥ śāstre nakṣatrataddevatādagdhayogādinirūpaṇam nāma ekonaṣaṣṭitamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam ṣaṣ āditye daśā jñeyā some pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ aṣṭau aṅgārake cava budhaiḥ spatadaśa smṛtāḥ śanaiścare daśa jñeyāḥ guroḥ ekonaviṃśatiḥ raveḥ daśā duḥ kha dā syāt udvega nṛpa nāśa kṛt vibhūti dā soma daśā sukham iṣṭa anna dā tathā duḥ kha pradā kuja daśā rājya ādeḥ syāt vināśinī divya strī dā budha daśā rājya dā kośa vṛddhi dā śaneḥ daśā rājya nāśa bandhu duḥkha karī bhavet guroḥ daśā rājya dā syāt sukha dharma ādi dāyinī rāhoḥ daśā rājya nāśa vyādhi dā duḥ kha dā bhavet hasti aśva dā śukra daśā rājya strī lābha dā bhavet meṣa aṅgāraka kṣetram vṛṣaḥ śukrasya kīrtitaḥ sūrya kṣetram bhavet siṃhaḥ kanyā kṣetram budhasya ca bhārgavasya tulā kṣetram vṛścika uṅgārakasya ca paurṇamāsyāḥ dvayam tatra pūrvāṣāḍhā dvayam bhavet dvirāṣāḍhaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ viṣṇuḥ svapiti karkaṭe aśvinī revatī citrā dhaniṣṭhā syāt alaṅkṛtau mṛga ahi kapi mārjāra śvānaḥ sūkara pakṣiṇaḥ nakulaḥ mūṣakaḥ ca eva yātrāyām dakṣiṇe śubhaḥ vipra kanyā śivā eṣām śaṅkha bherī vasundharāḥ veṇu strī pūrṇa kumbhāḥ ca yātrāyām darśanam śubham jambūka uṣṭra khara ādyāḥ ca yātrāyām vāmake śubhāḥ kārpāsa oṣadhi tailam ca pakra aṅgāra bhujaṅgamāḥ mukta keśī rakta mālya nagna ādya śubham īkṣitam lakṣaṇam vakṣye labhat pūrve mahā phalam āgneye śoka santāpau dakṣiṇe hānim āpnuyāt nairṛtya śoka santāpau miṣṭānnam ca eva paścime īśāne maraṇam proktam hikkāyāḥ ca phala aphalam hṛdaye pañca ṛkṣāṇi ekam nābhau pradāpayet nakṣatrāṇi ca śeṣāṇi ravi pāde niyojayet caraṇasyena ṛkṣeṇa alpa āyuḥ jāyate naraḥ nābhi sthena alpa santuṣṭaḥ hṛd sthena syāt maheśvaraḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍam prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe jyotiḥ śāstre grahadaśādinirūpaṇam nāma ṣaṣṭitamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam saṃpūjyamānaḥ lokaiḥ tu guru vat dṛśyate śaśī candrasya dvādaśa avasthāḥ bhavanti śṛṇu tāḥ api triṣu triṣu ca ṛkṣeṣu aśvinī ādi vadāmi aham pravāsa stham punar dṛṣṭam mṛta avastham jaya āvaham hāsya avastham nata a avastham pramoda avastham eva ca kampa avastham sukha avastham dvādaśa avastha gam bhavet pravāsaḥ ca jayaḥ hāseratiḥ sukham janma sthaḥ kurute tuṣṭim dvitīye nā asti nirvṛtiḥ tṛtīye rāja sanmānam caturthe kalaha āgamaḥ pañcamena mṛgāṅkena strī lābhaḥ vai tathā bhavet ghana dhānya āgamaḥ ṣaṣṭhe ratiḥ pūjā ca saptame aṣṭame prāṇa sandehaḥ navame kośa sañcayaḥ daśame kārya niṣpatti dhruvam ekādaśe jayaḥ dvādaśena śaśāṅkena mṛtyuḥ eva na saṃkhayaḥ kṛttikā ādau ca pūrveṇa sapta ṛkṣāṇi ca vai vrajet maghā ādau dakṣiṇe gacchet anurādhā ādi paścime praśastā ca uttara yātrā dhaniṣṭhā ādiṣu saptasu mṛga aśvi citrā puṣyāḥ ca mūlāḥ hastā śubhāḥ sadā kanyā pradāne yātrāyām pratiṣṭhā ādiṣu karmasu śaśi jña śukra jīvāḥ ca rāśau rāśau ca atha tṛtīyake bhauma manda śaśāṅka arkāḥ budhaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ caturthake jña śukrau aṣṭame śreṣṭhau navama sthaḥ guruḥ śubhaḥ arka ārki candrāḥ daśame grahāḥ ekādaśe khilāḥ siṃhena makaraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kanyayā meṣaḥ uttamaḥ dhanuṣā vṛṣabhaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ mithunena ca vṛścikaḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe jyotiḥ śāstre grahāṇām śubhāśubhasthānādinirūpaṇam nāma ekapaṣṭitamaḥ adhyāyaḥ iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe jyotiḥ śāstre lagnaghaṭikāpramāṇādinirūpaṇam nāma dviṣaṣṭitamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam nara strī lakṣaṇam vakṣye saṃkṣapāt śṛṇu śaṅkara kūrma unnatau ca caraṇau syātām nṛpa varasya hi virūkṣa pāṇḍura nakhau vakrau ca eva śira ānatau sūrpa ākārau ca caraṇau saṃkhuṣkau virala aṅgulī alpa roma yutā śreṣṭhā jaṅghā hasti kara upamā roma ekaikam kūpake syāt bhūpānām tu mahātmanām dve dve romṇī paṇḍitānām śrotriyāṇām tathā eva ca roma trayam daridrāṇām rogī nirmāṃsa jānukaḥ alpa liṅgī ca dhanavān syāt ca putra ādi varjitaḥ sthūla liṅgaḥ daridraḥ syāt dukhī eka vṛṣṇiḥ bhavet viṣame strī cañcalaḥ vai nṛpaḥ syāt vṛṣaṇe same pāṇḍuraiḥ malinaiḥ ca eva maṇibhiḥ ca sukhī naraḥ niḥ svāḥ sa śabda mūtrāḥ syuḥ nṛpāḥ niḥśabda dhārayā bhoga āḍhyāḥ sama jaṭharāḥ niḥ svāḥ syuḥ ghaṭa sannibhāḥ sarpa udarāḥ daridrāḥ syuḥ rekhābhiḥ ca āyuḥ ucyate lalāṭe yasya dṛśyante tisraḥ rekhāḥ samāhitāḥ sukhī putra samāyuktaḥ sa ṣaṣṭim jīvate naraḥ catvāriṃśat ca varṣāṇi dvi rekhā darśanāt naraḥ viṃśati abdam tu eka rekhāḥ ā karṇa antāḥ śata āyuṣaḥ saptati āyuḥ dvi rekhā tu ṣaṣṭi āyuḥ tisṛbhiḥ bhavet vyakta avyaktābhiḥ rekhābhiḥ viṃśati āyūḥ bhavet naraḥ catvāriṃśat ca varṣāṇi hīna rekhaḥ tu jīvati tarjanyāḥ madhyamā aṅgulyāḥ āyūrekhā tu madhyataḥ saṃprāptā yā bhavet rudra sa jīvet śaradaḥ śatam prathamā jñāna rekhā tu hi aṅguṣṭhāt anuvartate madhyamā ā mūla gā rekhā āyūrekhā atas param kaniṣṭhikām samāśritya āyūrekhā samāviśet a chinnā vā vibhaktā vā sa jīvet śaradaḥ śatam śata varṣāṇi jīvet ca bhogī rudra na saṃśayaḥ kaniṣṭhikām samāśritya madhyamāyām upāgatā kuryāt āyūrekhā tu mānavam iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe jyotiḥ śāstre sāmudrike puṃllakṣaṇanirūpaṇam nāma triṣaṣṭitamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam yasyāḥ tu kuñcitāḥ keśāḥ mukham ca parimaṇḍalam nābhiḥ ca dakṣiṇa āvartā sā kanyā kula vardhinī yā ca kāñcana varṇa ābhā rakta hasta saroruhā sahasrāṇām tu nārīṇām bhavet sā api pativratā vakra keśā ca yā kanyā maṇḍala akṣī ca yā bhavet bhartā ca mriyate tasyāḥ niyatam duḥ kha bhāginī pūrṇa candra mukhī kanyā bāla sūrya sama prabhā viśāla netrā bimboṣṭhī sā kanyā labhate sukham rekhābhiḥ bahubhiḥ kleśam su alpābhiḥ dhana hīna tā raktābhiḥ sukham āpnoti kṛṣṇābhiḥ preṣya tām vrajet kārye ca mantrī sat strī syāt satī syāt karaṇeṣu ca bhāryā mātā syāt veśyā ca śayane śubhā aṅkuśam kuṇḍalam cakram yasyāḥ pāṇi tale bhavet yasyāḥ pāṇi tale rekhā prākāraḥ toraṇam bhavet api dāsa kule jātā rājñī tvam upagacchati yasyāḥ anāmikā aṅguṣṭhau pṛthivyām na eva tiṣṭhataḥ patim mārayate kṣipram sva icchā ācāreṇa vartate patim mārayate kṣipram sva icchā ācāreṇa vartate tvacaḥ snehena śāyyām ca pāda snehena vāhanam matsya aṅkuśa abja cihnau ca cakra lāṅgala lakṣitau nābhiḥ praśastā gambhīrā dakṣiṇa āvartikā śubhā iti śrī gāruḍe mahāpurāṇe pūrva khaṇḍe prathama aṃśa ākhye ācāra kāṇḍe jyotiḥ śāstre sāmudrike strīlakṣaṇanirūpaṇam nāma catuḥṣaṣṭitamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī garuḍa mahāpurāṇam samudra uktam pravakṣyāmi nara strī lakṣaṇam śubham śleṣṭa aṅgulī tāmra nakhau pāda āviṣṇau śira ujjhitau kūrma unnatau gūḍha gulphau supārṣṇaḥ nṛpateḥ smṛtau śū pākārau virūkṣau ca vakrau pādau śira alakau saṃśuṣkau pāṇḍura nakhau niḥ svasya virala aṅgulī mārgāya utkaṇṭakau pādau kaṣāya sadṛśau tathā mṛdu romā samā jaṅghā tathā kari kara prabhā ūravaḥ jānavaḥ tulyāḥ nṛpasya upacitāḥ smṛtāḥ niḥ svasya sṛgāla jaṅghāḥ rauma ekaikam ca kūpake tri ādyaiḥ niḥ svāḥ mānavāḥ syuḥ dus svabhājaḥ ca ninditāḥ keśāḥ ca vai kuñcitāḥ ca pravāse mriyate naraḥ nirmāṃsa jānuḥ saubhāgyam alpaiḥ nimnaiḥ ratiḥ striyāḥ vikaṭaiḥ ca daridrāḥ syuḥ sa māṃsaiḥ rājyam eva ca mahadbhiḥ āyuḥ ākhyātam hi alpa liṅgaḥ dhanī naraḥ kośa gūḍhe dīrghaiḥ bhugnaiḥ ca dhana varjitaḥ balavān yuddha śīlaḥ ca saḥ eva ca durbalaḥ tu eka vṛṣaṇaḥ viṣama ābhyāñcalaḥ striyām samābhyām kṣitipaḥ proktaḥ pralambena śata abdavān tābhyām ca bahu āyuḥ rūkṣaiḥ maṇibhiḥ īśvaraḥ pāṇḍaraiḥ maṇibhiḥ niḥ svāḥ malinaiḥ sukha bhāginaḥ sa śabdaniḥ śabda mūtrāḥ syuḥ aṃridrāḥ ca mānavāḥ eka dvi tri catuḥ pañca ṣaḍbhiḥ dhārābhiḥ eva ca dakṣiṇa āvarta calita mūtrāḥ bhiḥ ca nṛpāḥ smṛtāḥ vikīrṇa mūtrāḥ svāḥ ca pradhāna sukha dāyikāḥ eka dhārāḥ ca vanitāḥ snigdhaiḥ maṇibhiḥ unnataiḥ śuṣkaiḥ niśvāḥ viśuṣkaiḥ ca durbhagāḥ parikīrtitāḥ puṣpa gandhe nṛpāḥ śukre madhu gandhe dhanam bahuḥ daridraḥ kṣāra gandhe ca dīrgha āyuḥ śīghra maithunī māṃsala sphic sukhī syāt ca siṃha sphic bhūpatiḥ smṛtaḥ bhavet siṃha kaṭī rājā niḥ svaḥ kapi kaṭiḥ naraḥ sarpa udarāḥ daridrāḥ syuḥ piṭharaiḥ ca ghaṭaiḥ samaiḥ sama kakṣāḥ ca bhoga āḍhyāḥ nimna kakṣāḥ dhana ujjhitāḥ nṛpāḥ ca unnata kakṣāḥ syuḥ jihnāḥ viṣama kakṣakāḥ matsya udarāḥ bahu dhanāḥ nābhibhiḥ sukhinaḥ smṛtāḥ bali madhya gatā nābhiḥ śūla bādhām karoti hi vāma āvartaḥ ca sādhyam vai medhām dakṣiṇatas tathā pārśva āyatā cira āyuḥ dā tu upaviṣṭā dhaneśvaram adhas gavāḍhyam kuryāt ca nṛpa tvam padma karṇikā eka baliḥ śata āyuḥ syāt śrī bhogī dvi valiḥ smṛtaḥ tri valiḥ kṣmāpaḥ ācāryaḥ sukhī agamyā gāmī jihma baliḥ bhūpāḥ pārśvaiḥ ca māṃsalaiḥ mṛdubhiḥ su samaiḥ ca eva dakṣiṇa āvarta romabhiḥ viparītaiḥ para preṣyāḥ nirdravyāḥ sukha varjitāḥ nirdhanāḥ viṣamaiḥ derghaiḥ pīta upacitakaiḥ nṛpāḥ sama unnatam ca hṛdayam akampam māṃsalam pṛthu nṛpāṇām adhamānām ca khara roma śirālakam arthavān sama vakṣāḥ syāt pīnaiḥ vakṣobhiḥ ūrjitaḥ vakṣobhiḥ viṣamaiḥ niḥ svar śastreṇanidhanāḥ tathā viṣamaiḥ jatrubhiḥ niḥ svāḥ asthi naddhaiḥ ca mānavāḥ unnataiḥ bhoginaḥ nimnaiḥ niḥ svāḥ pīnaiḥ dhana anvitāḥ niḥ svaḥ cipiṭa kaṇṭhaḥ syāt sirā śuṣka galaḥ sukhī śūraḥ syāt mahiṣa grīvaḥ śāstra āttaḥ mṛga kaṇṭhakaḥ kambu grīvaḥ ca nṛpatiḥ lamba kaṇṭhaḥ ati bhakṣakaḥ aromaśāḥ bhugna pṛṣṭham śubham ca aśubham anyathā kakṣā aśvattha dalā śreṣṭhā sugandhiḥ mṛga romikā anyathā tu artha hīnānām dāridrayasya ca kāraṇam ca eva bhugna alpau śliṣṭau ca vipulau śubhau ājānu lambitau bāhū vṛttau pīnau nṛpa īśvare niḥ svānām romaśau hrasvau śreṣṭhau kari kara prabhau hasta aṅgulayaḥ eva syuḥ vāyu dvāra yutāḥ śubhāḥ medhāvinām ca sūkṣmāḥ syuḥ bhṛtyānām cipiṭāḥ smṛtāḥ sthūla aṅgulībhiḥ niḥ svāḥ syuḥ natāḥ syuḥ su kṛśaiḥ tadā pitṛ vitta vināśaḥ ca nimnāt kara talāt narāḥ maṇibandhaiḥ nigūḍhaiḥ ca su śliṣṭaiḥ śubha gandhibhiḥ nṛpāḥ hīnāḥ kara chaidaiḥ sa śabdaiḥ dhana varjitāḥ saṃvṛtaiḥ ca eva nimnaiḥ ca dhaninaḥ parikīrtitāḥ prottānaka ra dātāraḥ viṣamaiḥ viṣamāḥ narāḥ karaiḥ kara talaiḥ ca eva lākṣā bhaiḥ īśvarāḥ talaiḥ paradāra ratāḥ pītaiḥ ūkṣaiḥ niḥ svāḥ narāḥ matāḥ tuṣa tulya nakhāḥ klībāḥ kuṭilaiḥ sphuṭitaiḥ narāḥ niḥ svāḥ ca kunakhaiḥ tadvat vivarṇaiḥ paratarkakāḥ tāmraiḥ bhūpāḥ dhana āḍhyāḥ ca aṅguṣṭhaiḥ sa yavaiḥ tathā aṅguṣṭha mūla jaiḥ putrī syāt dīrgha aṅguli parvakaḥ dīrgha āyuḥ subhagaḥ ca eva nirdhanaḥ virala aṅguliḥ nṛpateḥ kara tala gāḥ maṇibandhāt samutthitāḥ vajra ākārāḥ ca dhaninām matsya puccha nibhāḥ budhe śaṅkha ātapatra śivikā gaja padma upamāḥ nṛpe kumbha aṅkuśa patākā ābhāḥ mṛṇāla ābhāḥ nidhi īśvare dāma ābhāḥ ca gavā āḍhyānām svastika ābhāḥ nṛpa īśvare cakra asi tomara dhanuḥ kunta ābhāḥ nṛpateḥ kare alu ukhala ābhāḥ yajña āḍhyāḥ vedī bhā ca agnihotriṇi vāpī deva kulyā ābhāḥ trikoṇa ābhāḥ ca dhārmike aṅguṣṭha mūla gāḥ rekhāḥ putrāḥ sūkṣmāḥ ca dārikāḥ pradeśinī gatā rekhā kaniṣṭhā mūla gāminī niḥ svāḥ ca bahu rekhāḥ syuḥ nirdravyāḥ cibukaiḥ kṛśaiḥ māṃsalaiḥ ca dhana upetāḥ āraktaiḥ adharaiḥ nṛpāḥ bimba upamaiḥ ca sphuṭitaiḥ oṣṭhaiḥ ūkṣaiḥ cakaṇḍitaiḥ viṣamaiḥ dhana hīnāḥ ca dantāḥ snigdhāḥ ghanāḥ śubhāḥ tīkṣṇāḥ dantāḥ samāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ jihvā raktā samā śubhā ślakṣṇā dīrghā ca vijñeyā tālū śvete dhana kṣaye kṛṣṇe ca paruṣaḥ vakram samam saumyam ca saṃvṛtam bhūpānām amalam ślakṣṇam viparītam ca duḥ khinām mahā duḥ kham durbhagāṇām strī mukham putram āpnuyāt āḍhyānām vartulam vakram nirdravyāṇām ca dīrghakam bhīru vakraḥ pāpa karmā dhūrtānām caturaśrakam nimnam vakram aputrāṇām kṛpaṇānām ca hrasvakam sampūrṇam bhoginām kāntam śmaśru snigdham śubham mṛdu saṃhatam ca a sphuṭita agram rakta śmaśruḥ ca caurakaḥ rakta alpa paruṣa śmaśru karṇāḥ syuḥ pāpa mṛtyavaḥ nirmāṃsaiḥ cipiṭaiḥ bhogāḥ kṛpaṇāḥ hrasva karṇakāḥ śaṅku karṇāḥ ca rājānaḥ roma karṇāḥ gata āyuṣaḥ karṇaiḥ snigdha avanaddhaiḥ ca vyālambaiḥ māṃsalaiḥ nṛpāḥ śuka nāsaḥ sukhī syāt ca śuṣka nāsaḥ ati jīvanaḥ chinna agra kūpa nāsaḥ syāt agamyā gamane rataḥ dīrgha nāse ca saubhāgyam cauraḥ ca ākuñcita indriyaḥ mṛtyuḥ cipiṭa nāse syāt hīnaḥ bhāgyavatām bhavet krūre dakṣiṇa vakrā syāt valinām ca kṣutam sakṛt syāt viniṣpiṇḍitam hrādi sa anunādam ca jīva kṛt mārjāra locanaiḥ pāpmā durātmā madhu piṅgalaiḥ krūrāḥ kekara netrāḥ ca harita akṣāḥ sa kalmaṣāḥ jihyaiḥ ca locanaiḥ śūrāḥ senānyaḥ gaja locanāḥ gambhīra akṣāḥ īśvarāḥ syuḥ mantriṇaḥ sthūla cakṣuṣaḥ lākṣāḥ vidvāṃsaḥ saubhāgyam śyāma cakṣuṣām syāt kṛṣṇa tāraka akṣāṇām akṣṇām utpāṭanam kila maṇḍala akṣāḥ ca pāpāḥ syuḥ niḥ svāḥ syuḥ viśāla unnatā sukhinī daridrā viṣama bhruvaḥ āḍhyaḥ niḥ svaḥ ca khaṇḍa bhrṛḥ madhye ca vinata bhruvaḥ strīṣu gamyāsu saktāḥ syuḥ suta arthe parivarjitāḥ an nataiḥ vipulaiḥ śaṅkhaiḥ lalāṭaiḥ viṣamaiḥ tathā ācāryāḥ śukti viśālaiḥ śirālaiḥ pāpa kāriṇaḥ sirābhiḥ ca svastikābhiḥ dhana īśvarāḥ nimnaiḥ lalāṭaiḥ bandha arhāḥ krūra karma ratāḥ tathā saṃvṛtaiḥ ca lalāṭaiḥ ca kṛpaṇāḥ unnataiḥ nṛpāḥ anaśru snigdha ruditam adīnam śubha dam nṛṇām pracura aśru dīnam rūkṣam ca ruditam ca sukha āvaham akampam hasitam śreṣṭham mīlita akṣam agha āvaham asakṛt hasitam duṣṭam sa unmādasya hi anekadhā lalāṭa upasṛtāḥ tisraḥ rekhāḥ syuḥ śata varṣiṇām nṛpa tvam syāt catasṛbhiḥ āyuḥ pañcanavati atha arekheṇa āyuḥ navatiḥ vicchinnābhiḥ ca keśānta upagatābhiḥ ca aśīti āyūḥ naraḥ bhavet pañcabhiḥ saptabhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ pañcāśat vahubhiḥ tathā catvāriṃśat ca vakrābhiḥ triṃśat bhrū lagna gāmibhiḥ viṃśatiḥ vāma vakrāḥ bhiḥ āyuḥ kṣudrābhiḥ alpakam chatra ākāraiḥ śirobhiḥ tu nṛpāḥ nimna śirāḥ dhanī cipiṭaiḥ ca pituḥ mṛtyuḥ go ādyāḥ parimaṇḍalaiḥ ghaṭa mūrdhā pāpa ruciḥ dhana ādyaiḥ parivarjitaḥ kṛṣṇaiḥ ākuñcitaiḥ keśaiḥ snigdhaiḥ ekaika sambhavaiḥ abhinna agraiḥ ca mṛdubhiḥ na ca ati bahubhiḥ nṛpāḥ bahu mūlaiḥ ca viṣamaiḥ sthūla agraiḥ kapilaiḥ tathā yat yat gātram mahā rūkṣam śirālam māṃsa varjitam tat tat syāḥ daśubham sarvam tatas anyathā vipulaḥ triṣu gambhīraḥ dīrghaḥ sūkṣmaḥ ca pañcasu nābhiḥ svaraḥ ca sa sattvam ca trayam gambhīram īritam puṃsaḥ syāt ati vistīrṇam lalāṭam vadanam hi uraḥ cakṣuḥ kakṣāḥ nāsikā ca ṣaṭ syuḥ nṛpa kṛkāṭikāḥ unnatāni ca hrasvani jaṅghā grīvā ca liṅgakam pṛṣṭham catvāri raktāni kara tālu adharāḥ nakhāḥ netra anta pāda jihvā oṣṭhāḥ pañca sūkṣmāṇi santi vai dīrghāḥ stanāntaram bāhu danta locana nāsikāḥ śliṣṭa aṅgulī ca unnata agrau tām pāpya nṛpatiḥ bhavet anulbaṇam sandhi deśam samam jānu dvayam śubham ūrū kari kara ākārau aromau ca samau śubhau śroṇī lalāṭakam strīṇām ūru kūrma unnatam śubham gūḍhaḥ maṇiḥ ca śubha daḥ nitambaḥ ca guruḥ śubhaḥ vistīrṇa māṃsa upacitā gambhīrā vipulā śubhā nābhiḥ pradakṣiṇa āvartā madhyam tri bali śobhitam kaṭhinau romaśā śastā mṛdu grīvā ca kambu bhā āraktau adharau śreṣṭhau māṃsalam vartulam mukham kunda puṣpa samāḥ dantāḥ bhāṣitam kokilā samam dākṣiṇya yuktam aśaṭham haṃsa śabda sukha āvaham nāsā samā sama puṭā strīṇām tu rucirā śubhā na pṛthū bāla indu nibhe bhruvau ca atha lalāṭakam śubham ardha indu saṃsthānam atuṅgam syāt alomaśam su māṃsalam karṇa yugmam samam mṛdu samāhitam snigdhāḥ nīlāḥ ca mṛdavaḥ mūrdhajāḥ kuñcitāḥ kacāḥ strīṇām samam śiraḥ śreṣṭham pāde pāṇi tale atha vā vāji kuñjara śrīvṛkṣa yūpa iṣu yava tomaraiḥ dhvaja cāmara mālābhiḥ śaila kuṇḍala vedibhiḥ lakṣaṇaiḥ aṅkuśa ādyaiḥ ca striyaḥ syuḥ rāja vallabhāḥ na nimnam nā unnatam strīṇām bhavet kara talam śubham rekhā anvitam tu avidhavām kuryāt saṃbhoginīm striyam rekhā yā maṇibandha utthā gatā madhyāṅgulim kare gatā pāṇi tale yā ca yā ūrdhva pāda tale sthitā strīṇām puṃsām tathā sā syāt rājyāya ca sukhāya ca kaniṣṭhikā mūla bhavā rekhā kuryāt śata āyuṣam pradeśinī madhyamābhyām antarāla gatā satī ūnāḥ ūna āyuṣam kuryāt rekhāḥ ca aṅguṣṭha mūla gāḥ bṛhatyaḥ putrāḥ tanvyaḥ tu pramadāḥ parikīrtitāḥ su alpa āyuṣaḥ bahu chinnāḥ dīrgha a chinnāḥ mahā āyuṣam śubham tu lakṣaṇam strīṇām proktam tu aśubham anyathā kaniṣṭhikā anāmikā vā yasyāḥ na spṛśate mahīm aṅguṣṭham vā gatā atītya tarjanī kulaṭā ca sā ūrdhvam dvābhyām piṇḍikābhyām jaṅghe ca ati śirālake romaśe ca ati māṃse ca kumbha ākāram tathā udaram vāma āvartam nimnam alpam duḥ khitānām ca guhyakam grīvayā hrasvayā niḥ svāḥ dīrghayā ca kula kṣayaḥ pṛthulayā pracaṇḍāḥ ca striyaḥ syuḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ kekare piṅgale netre śyāme lola īkṣaṇā satī smite kūpe gaṇḍayoḥ ca sā dhruvam vyabhicāriṇī pralambinī lalāṭe tu devaram hanti ca aṅganā yā tu roma uttara oṣṭhī syāt na śubhā bhartuḥ eva hi karālāḥ viṣamāḥ dantāḥ kleśāya ca bhavanti te cauryāya kṛṣṇa māṃsāḥ ca dīrghāḥ bhurtuḥ ca mṛtyave kravyāda rūpaiḥ hastaiḥ ca vṛka kāka ādi sannibhaiḥ śirālaiḥ viṣamaiḥ śuṣkaiḥ vitta hīnāḥ bhavanti hi samunnata uttareṣṭhī yā kalahe rūkṣa bhāṣiṇī strīṣu doṣāḥ
